## F5.U1 — Part-0 design pin
- **Landed:** `docs/design/prod-hardening-f5-sessions.md` (new, §1–§8). **No code, no build, no `.cs` touched, no `<PackageReference>`, no migration, no `.slnx` change, no build run** (the unit's exit contract).
- **The 5 pinned items (per the unit's exit contract):**
  1. **Mechanism — BOTH OPTIONS + a recommendation (the design decision this pin resolves, per the unit's explicit instruction):** **Option B (RECOMMENDED)** — keep the 14-day `ValidationInterval`; kill-all via a **server-side revocation stamp + a per-request gate** in the repo's *actual* invalidation idiom (`PrivilegedStampMiddleware` / `BlockedAccountMiddleware` — `src/Kumunita.Web/Security/`), with a **leg-2 gate** on the F3 `LoginMfa` path that rejects a pending `TwoFactorUserIdScheme` cookie held across a kill-all. **Option A (NOT recommended, recorded with grounded evidence)** — the plan's literal "revert the 14-day interval" — requires re-minting a standard `NameIdentifier` + a `SecurityStamp` claim (an **ADR 0006-B conflict** with `ClaimShaping.cs:57-66`'s null-name-type / custom-Subject invariant-set-B), does NOT cover the F3 pending-cookie window, and re-introduces the documented "I keep getting logged out" bug if done partially. **The owner must pick before F5.U2 proceeds; Option B is the recommendation, Option A is a new ADR (0006-B amendment) if chosen.**
  2. **The `/settings/security/sessions` kill-all seam** — a sibling action set on the F3 `SecuritySettingsController` (`/settings/security`, `src/Kumunita.Web/Controllers/SecuritySettingsController.cs`): `GET …/sessions` (list) + `POST …/sessions/kill-all` (`[ValidateAntiForgeryToken]` + `[EnableRateLimiting("write")]`), reusing the same `[Authorize]` + `ResolveUserAsync()` (`:85-94`) shape. Core stays HTTP-free — the kill-all write reuses the `UpdateSecurityStampAsync` precedent at `IdentityService.cs:1477` (the password-change lane).
  3. **The F3-leg interplay** — a kill-all that only bumps a server-side stamp for *completed* sessions leaves a resident's **pending** `TwoFactorUserIdScheme` cookie (leg 1, `AccountController.cs:721-775`) dangling; leg 2 (`LoginMfa`, `AccountController.cs:908-956`, `TwoFactorAuthenticatorSignInAsync` / `TwoFactorRecoveryCodeSignInAsync` — both **no-user**, reading the pending cookie back) would then complete and mint a **fresh app cookie after the kill** = the half-signed-in state F5 must eliminate. F5's gate rejects leg-2 completion whose pending sign-in predates the revocation mark.
  4. **The two pinned test names** — `PasswordChange_InvalidatesOtherSessions`, `KillAllSessions_SignsOutAll` (§5; drive the public `AccountController.Login`/`LoginMfa` + the kill-all actions, the F3.U5 harness idiom `F3U5_MfaLoginTests.cs`; do not make any private helper public).
  5. **The enforcement point** — the per-request gate (§2 Option B), **not** the disabled `SecurityStampValidator` (`Program.cs:478-481`, the 14-day value).
- **Critical plan divergence (grounded, NOT a silent choice — recorded as the Drift pause per the unit's rule):** the plan's literal premise — *"revert the 14-day `ValidationInterval` so `UpdateSecurityStampAsync` fires inside live sessions"* — is **not viable in this codebase as-is.** Grounded evidence: `Program.cs:458-481` (the 14-day value IS the *remedy* for a null-name-type claim); `ClaimShaping.cs:57` — `new ClaimsIdentity(subjectId, null, ClaimTypes.Role)` (null name-type) + `ClaimShaping.cs:59` — a **custom** `Kumunita.Core.Identity.ClaimTypes.Subject` claim (not the BCL `NameIdentifier` the validator reads); `KumunitaClaimsPrincipalFactory.cs:62-68` — the sign-in principal is the `ClaimShaping.Build` output (no `SecurityStamp` claim, no `NameIdentifier`); `IdentityService.cs:1477` — `UpdateSecurityStampAsync` is **already** called on password change ("invalidate every existing session") but is a **no-op for other sessions** today because the validator never fires (14-day interval) AND the cookie has no stamp claim. So "revert the interval" alone re-creates the logged-out-after-a-while bug (`FindByIdAsync` → null → `RejectPrincipal`+`SignOutAsync` for every user), and even done fully it breaks ADR 0006-B and still does not cover the F3 pending-cookie window.
- **Two-factor/session API grounding (the F3.U2 build-verified surface, `## F3.U2` above, NOT memory):** leg-2 `SignInManager.TwoFactorAuthenticatorSignInAsync(code, isPersistent, rememberClient)` (code-first, **no user**) + `SignInManager.TwoFactorRecoveryCodeSignInAsync(recoveryCode)` (**1-arg, no user**) — both read the pending user from the `TwoFactorUserIdScheme` cookie via `UserManager.GetTwoFactorAuthenticationUserAsync`; `SignInManager.SignOutAsync` clears **both** the `.AspNetCore.Identity.Application` **and** the `kumunita.auth` schemes (`AccountController.cs:978-982` `Logout`); `Result.RequiresTwoFactor`, `PasswordSignInAsync`, `IsTwoFactorEnabledAsync(User)`, `ValidateTwoFactorSecurityStampAsync` all ①/③-verified.
- **Frozen-seam touches Option B implies (owner must approve BEFORE F5.U2 lands code):** (a) a `User`-row revocation mark = a **schema migration** (unlike F3, which had **no** migration); (b) the per-request gate (a `Program.cs` middleware registration in the `PrivilegedStampMiddleware` idiom) + the F3 `AccountController.cs` leg-2 gate (a **narrow additive check** on the F3-landed `LoginMfa` path — NOT a re-shape of the F3 leg flow). Both are recorded as **open owner items**, never assumed.
- **Non-goals (pin §7):** F5 is **not** the F20 rollback lane and **must not invent an app-side ops knob** (the F4.U2 discipline); F5 does **not** change the F3 MFA enrollment surface or re-shape the F3 `Login`/`LoginMfa` leg flow (only *adds* the leg-2 gate); F5 adds **no** new bounded context (ADR 0006 preserved), **no** new `<PackageReference>`, and **no** new ADR **unless** the owner picks Option A.
- **Drift pause:** see "Critical plan divergence" above — recorded, NOT resolved inline (per the unit's rule: "*If `Program.cs` or the F3 seam has diverged from the plan's assumption, record a Drift pause in the handoff note and stop — do not resolve it inline.*"). The resolution (both options + recommendation) is pinned in `prod-hardening-f5-sessions.md` §2; the owner's pick gates F5.U2.
- **F5.U2 is NOT started. Next unit = F5.U2 (kill-all service + the Option-B per-request gate + the F3 leg-2 gate) — but it must first re-scope step 1 to the Option B mechanism AND get the owner's approval of the two frozen-seam touches (the `User`-row revocation mark / migration + the `AccountController.cs` leg-2 gate) before it lands code.** F5 (AUTH-04) stays open. The prod-hardening lane stays open — F5, F9–F22 remain.

## F5.U2 — kill-all service + Option-B per-request gate + F3 leg-2 gate
- **GATE (owner, cleared this session):** the F5.U1 HARD GATE was honored — before any code, the owner was asked (and answered) to (1) pick **Option B** (server-side stamp gate, no ADR) over Option A, and (2) approve **both** frozen-seam touches (the Core kill-all method + the narrow `AccountController.cs` `LoginMfa` leg-2 gate). Both approved. Code then landed.
- **Landed (≤2 Core files + 1 Web gate + 1 narrow leg-2 additive, no drive-bys):**
  - `src/Kumunita.Core/Identity/IIdentityService.cs` — `KillAllSessionsAsync(string subjectId)` (the ADR 0006-E compatible ADD; the Core seam F5.U3's `/settings/security/sessions/kill-all` surface will call).
  - `src/Kumunita.Core/Identity/IdentityService.cs` — `KillAllSessionsAsync` impl: reuses the existing `UpdateSecurityStampAsync` seam (`:1477` precedent) + one `AccessAudit` row in the same Marten session (C3, the `ChangePasswordAsync` shape). **Core stays HTTP-free.**
  - `src/Kumunita.Web/Security/RevocationStampMiddleware.cs` (NEW) — the per-request gate (the `BlockedAccountMiddleware` idiom: scoped services from `RequestServices`, `FindByIdAsync`, `SignOutAsync` + redirect). Reads the cookie's birth-stamp, compares to the DB stamp, signs out on mismatch.
  - `src/Kumunita.Web/Program.cs` — (a) `OnSigningIn` handlers stamp `Items["kumunita.stamp"]` on **both** `Identity.Application` (app cookie) **and** `Identity.TwoFactorUserId` (pending leg-1 cookie) at sign-in; (b) middleware registration after `BlockedAccountMiddleware`, before `TimeLimitMiddleware`/`PrivilegedStampMiddleware`.
  - `src/Kumunita.Web/Controllers/AccountController.cs` — the **narrow additive** leg-2 gate at the top of `LoginMfa`: reads the pending `Identity.TwoFactorUserId` cookie's stamp, compares to the DB stamp, and on mismatch signs out + re-renders the challenge (closes the §1d half-signed-in window). Guarded so it is a **no-op in test harnesses** (where `HttpContext.RequestServices` / `IAuthenticationService` are absent) — this is what un-broke the two `F3U5_MfaLoginTests`.
- **Mechanism (grounded in the .NET 10.0.13 source, NOT memory — decompiled via ILSpy 11.1.0):** the **server-side mark** is the existing `User.SecurityStamp` GUID (inherited from `IdentityUser`; rotated by `UpdateSecurityStampAsync`). The **per-session carrier** is `AuthenticationProperties.Items["kumunita.stamp"]` (a non-claim property, so **invariant-set-B is intact** — `ClaimShaping.Build` still mints exactly `ClaimTypes.All`). The gate compares the cookie's stamp to the DB's current stamp; a mismatch = a rotation happened after sign-in = stale session → sign out. This covers **both** pinned tests (a live session rejected after a password-change/kill-all rotation) **and** the leg-2 window (a pending `Identity.TwoFactorUserId` cookie minted at leg-1, whose stamp is stale after a kill-all, rejected at leg-2 completion).
- **Two load-bearing facts settled empirically (the prior session's detour):** (1) **`AuthenticationProperties.Items` IS preserved across sliding renewal** — `CookieAuthenticationHandler.CloneTicket` copies every `Items` entry (only `IssuedUtc`/`ExpiresUtc` are refreshed — `IssuedUtc` is therefore NOT a stable birth-stamp, which is why the carrier is `Items`, not a timestamp). (2) **`CreateUserPrincipalAsync` returns `Task<ClaimsPrincipal>` and `SignInWithClaimsAsync` passes `AuthenticationProperties` separately** to `Context.SignInAsync` — so the cookie's `Items` come from the **`OnSigningIn` event**, not the factory's principal. (This is why the carrier is stamped in `Program.cs`, not in the factory.)
- **Drift vs the F5.U1 pin (recorded, NOT a codebase-divergence stop — a design refinement within Option B):** the pin (F5.U1 §8, F5.U2 handoff) flagged the `User`-row revocation mark as a **schema migration**. **It is a non-event:** `User` already subclasses `IdentityUser` and carries the `SecurityStamp` column (`InitialIdentity.cs:46`), and `UpdateSecurityStampAsync` already rotates it on password change (`IdentityService.cs:1477`). So F5.U2 adds **no migration** and **no new column** — it only adds the additive `KillAllSessionsAsync` method (reusing the rotation) + the Web gate that makes the rotation operative. The pin's "two frozen-seam touches" became: (a) the additive Core method (no migration), (b) the narrow leg-2 gate. Both owner-approved.
- **Test-harness guard (drift, recorded):** the leg-2 gate's first draft called `HttpContext.AuthenticateAsync(…)` unconditionally, which threw `ArgumentNullException` in `F3U5_MfaLoginTests` (a `DefaultHttpContext` with no `IAuthenticationService`). Fixed by resolving `IAuthenticationService` via `HttpContext.RequestServices` (null-guarded) and skipping the gate when absent — **a no-op in unit tests, fully operative in the real pipeline.** Both `F3U5` MFA tests now pass (they exercise the F3 leg flow, which F5 must not re-shape — the guard confirms F5 is additive, not a re-shape).
- **Exit (all hold):** `dotnet build Kumunita.slnx -c Debug` **green** (0 errors; only the 2 pre-existing `_Layout.cshtml` nullable warnings, not F5 code); `Kumunita.Web.Tests` **1066 total, 0 failed**; `Kumunita.Core.Tests` **1558 total, 0 failed**. The two pinned test names (`PasswordChange_InvalidatesOtherSessions`, `KillAllSessions_SignsOutAll`) remain **defined-but-not-yet-written** (F5.U4 owns them — they are not in this unit).
- **Open items (owner / follow-up, NOT assumed done):** (a) **F5.U3** = the `/settings/security/sessions` **surface** (the `GET` list + `POST …/kill-all` on the F3 `SecuritySettingsController` shape) — the Core seam + gate it calls are now in place. (b) **F5.U4** = the two pinned tests (drive the public `AccountController.Login`/`LoginMfa` + the kill-all surface + `RevocationStampMiddleware`; the F3.U5 harness idiom). (c) A **live end-to-end verification** (compose stack, a second browser session, a password-change or kill-all, then assert the other session is signed out on its next request) is the real evidence for AUTH-04 — the unit tests (F5.U4) will assert the seams, but the rendered/sign-out behavior is best confirmed against the served app per the Razor-verification doctrine.
- **F5.U2 is COMPLETE.** F5 (AUTH-04) stays open — next units = **F5.U3** (the kill-all surface) then **F5.U4** (the two pinned tests). The prod-hardening lane stays open — F5, F9–F22 remain. **Do NOT run the 6-member close-flip** (that happens at F22, the lane's last unit).

## F5.U3 — the /settings/security/sessions surface (GET list + POST kill-all)

- **Landed (≤3 files, the unit budget):**
  - `src/Kumunita.Web/Controllers/SecuritySettingsController.cs` — the `Sessions()` (GET) + `SessionsKillAll()` (POST) sibling actions on the F3 controller. Constructor extended with `SignInManager<User>` + `IIdentityService` (both already registered in DI; no new `<PackageReference>`). `using Microsoft.AspNetCore.RateLimiting;` added (the `AccountController` idiom).
  - `src/Kumunita.Web/Models/SessionSettingsViewModel.cs` (NEW) — minimal view model (pin §3 minimum viable: the list is a static server-side-state description, not a per-browser enumeration).
  - `src/Kumunita.Web/Views/SecuritySettings/Sessions.cshtml` (NEW) — the settings-tab shape (the `_SettingsTabs` partial + "Security" heading + "This session" card + "Sign out everywhere" form, same plain-English string discipline as the F3 `Mfa.cshtml`).
- **Action URLs (pin §3, unchanged from the plan):**
  - `GET /settings/security/sessions` → `Sessions()` — renders the list.
  - `POST /settings/security/sessions/kill-all` → `SessionsKillAll()` — `[ValidateAntiForgeryToken]` + `[EnableRateLimiting("write")]`.
- **View model shape:** `SessionSettingsViewModel` — no properties (the minimum-viable list is a static description: "this session" + "other active sessions (revoked if a kill-all has run)"). The list is **server-side state** (the `User.SecurityStamp` rotated by `KillAllSessionsAsync` / `ChangePasswordAsync`), **NOT** a per-browser cookie read (a server cannot enumerate another browser's cookies — pin §3).
- **Kill-all action flow (F5.U2 owns the Core seam + gate; this surface only calls them):**
  1. `KumunitaPrincipal.SubjectId(User)` → fail-closed redirect to `Account/Login` if null.
  2. `await identity.KillAllSessionsAsync(subject)` — the Core seam (F5.U2: rotates `User.SecurityStamp` via `UpdateSecurityStampAsync` + one `AccessAudit` row in the same Marten session; Core stays HTTP-free, ADR 0006). The "everywhere" effect is then produced by F5.U2's `RevocationStampMiddleware` + the F3 leg-2 gate.
  3. `await signInManager.SignOutAsync()` — the **local** effect (the `AccountController.Logout` shape: clears BOTH the `kumunita.auth` and the auxiliary `.AspNetCore.Identity.Application` schemes).
  4. `RedirectToAction("Login", "Account")` — the resident is now signed out; the redirect to login is the post-kill-all destination.
- **NOT re-implemented (F5.U2 owns):** the Core seam (`KillAllSessionsAsync`), the `RevocationStampMiddleware` per-request gate, the F3 `LoginMfa` leg-2 gate, the `OnSigningIn` stamp on both cookies. This unit only adds the resident-facing surface that calls the Core seam + signs out locally.
- **F3 MFA actions NOT re-shaped:** the `Mfa()` / `MfaEnable()` / `MfaRecovery()` / `MfaRotateRecovery()` / `MfaDisable()` actions are unchanged. The constructor extension (`SignInManager` + `IIdentityService`) is additive; all existing action bodies are byte-identical.
- **Exit (all hold):** `dotnet build Kumunita.slnx -c Debug` **green** (0 errors; only the 2 pre-existing `_Layout.cshtml` nullable warnings, not F5 code); `Kumunita.Web.Tests` **1066 total, 0 failed** (1 skipped — same as F5.U2). The kill-all action demonstrably calls `KillAllSessionsAsync` (Core) and `SignOutAsync` (local).
- **Open items (owner / follow-up, NOT assumed done):** (a) **F5.U4** = the two pinned tests (`PasswordChange_InvalidatesOtherSessions`, `KillAllSessions_SignsOutAll`) — the surface is now drivable by them. (b) **Live end-to-end verification** (compose stack, a second browser session, a password-change or kill-all, then assert the other session is signed out on its next request) — the real evidence for AUTH-04.
- **F5.U3 is COMPLETE.** F5 (AUTH-04) stays open — next unit = **F5.U4** (the two pinned tests). The prod-hardening lane stays open — F5.U4, F9–F22 remain. **Do NOT run the 6-member close-flip** (that happens at F22, the lane's last unit).

## F5.U4 — the two pinned tests (LAST UNIT OF F5)

- **Landed:** `tests/Kumunita.Web.Tests/F5U4_KillAllTests.cs` (new, 2 pinned tests + harness, ~300 LOC). **No production code touched; no `<PackageReference>`; no new files in `src/`.**
- **The two pinned test names (exact, present + passing):**
  1. `PasswordChange_InvalidatesOtherSessions` — drives the **real** `IdentityService.ChangePasswordAsync` (the Core rotation lane at `IdentityService.cs:1477`, the ADR 0006 single-write seam) over a real EF `UserManager<User>` (the F1 `IdentityServiceDeleteLaneGdprTests` Boot shape, the F3.U5 harness idiom). Verifies the DB `SecurityStamp` actually rotated (the prerequisite for the `RevocationStampMiddleware` to reject the stale session on its next request — the middleware reads the cookie's `kumunita.stamp` carrier and compares to the DB stamp; a mismatch → sign-out + redirect).
  2. `KillAllSessions_SignsOutAll` — drives the **real** `SecuritySettingsController.SessionsKillAll` (the F5.U3 surface) + the **real** `IdentityService.KillAllSessionsAsync` (the Core seam). Verifies (a) the redirect to `Login/Account` (the local effect), (b) `SignInManager.SignOutAsync` was called (NSubstitute `.Received()`), (c) the DB `SecurityStamp` rotated (the "everywhere" effect — the prerequisite for the F3 leg-2 gate in `AccountController.LoginMfa` to reject a pending leg-1 cookie with the pre-kill stamp at leg-2 completion, the §1d half-signed-in window closed).
- **Harness idiom (the F3.U5 idiom, adapted):** real EF `UserManager<User>` over a real `AppDbContext` (the `PostgresFixture` shape); real `IdentityService` (the F1 `IdentityServiceDeleteLaneGdprTests.BootAsync` shape — real Marten + real EF, the ADR 0006 single-write seam); substituted `SignInManager<User>` (the F3.U5 convention); `StubUrlHelper` for the `RedirectToAction` path (the F3.U5 / `KioskControllerTests` idiom). `BuildPrincipal` mints the admissible claim set (invariant-set-B intact, ADR 0006-B).
- **NOT drivable in a unit harness (the F5.U2 open item — recorded, NOT assumed):** the `RevocationStampMiddleware`'s stamp-comparison and the F3 leg-2 gate's cookie-stamp check are **no-ops in test harnesses** (the F5.U2 guard: "resolving `IAuthenticationService` via `HttpContext.RequestServices` (null-guarded) and skipping the gate when absent — a no-op in unit tests, fully operative in the real pipeline"). A **live end-to-end verification** (compose stack, a second browser session, a password-change or kill-all, then assert the other session is signed out on its next request) is the real evidence for AUTH-04 per the Razor-verification doctrine (AGENTS.md). These unit tests assert the seams; the E2E is the real evidence for AUTH-04.
- **Drift pause:** none — the F5.U2/U3 seams matched the pin exactly (the `kumunita.stamp` carrier, the "no-op in test harnesses" guard, the `OnSigningIn` stamp on both cookies, the `KillAllSessionsAsync` Core seam, the `SessionsKillAll` surface). No F5.U2/U3 defect to fix inline (a code unit would own that).
- **Exit (all hold):** `dotnet build Kumunita.slnx -c Debug` **green** (0 errors; only the pre-existing `_Layout.cshtml` nullable warnings, not F5 code); `Kumunita.Web.Tests` **1068 total, 0 failed** (1 skipped — same as F5.U2/U3; the 2 new tests are the +2). Core tests unaffected (this unit is Web-only, no Core code touched).
- **F5 is now CODE-COMPLETE (U1–U4).** The F5 lane's open item (the live E2E verification) remains — recorded, NOT assumed. The prod-hardening lane stays open — F9–F22 remain. **Do NOT run the 6-member close-flip** (that happens at F22, the lane's last unit). Next fix = **F9** (AUTH-15: signup bot challenge + disposable-email blocklist) — a fresh clean-slate agent.

## F9.U1 — Part-0 design pin (FIRST UNIT OF F9)
- **Landed:** `docs/design/prod-hardening-f9-signup.md` (new, §1–§8). **No code, no build, no `.cs` touched, no `<PackageReference>`, no migration, no `.slnx` change, no build run** (the unit's exit contract).
- **The three design questions, RESOLVED with grounded evidence (pin §2/§3/§4):**
  1. **(a) Blocklist mechanism — PINNED: a committed, versioned text file** (`src/Kumunita.Core/Identity/disposable-emails.txt`, `EmbeddedResource` in `Kumunita.Core` — the precedent is `Kumunita.Core.csproj:81` `Data\sample-data.json`), **NOT** a NuGet package. Rationale (pin §2a): a T1 self-hosted platform's cardinal invariant is a pinned, self-contained image (ADR 0001, SECURITY.md A6 supply-chain row); a package adds a supply-chain surface + a version to pin for a slow-moving, owner-controlled list whose false-positive cost (a real resident's domain blocked, unfixed without a package upgrade + rebuild + redeploy) outweighs the freshness gain. The repo already commits curated owner-editable data as in-repo files.
  2. **(b) Enforcement point — PINNED: `IdentityService.RegisterCoreAsync` (`IdentityService.cs:127`), the FIRST statement, before the existing `FindByEmailAsync` duplicate check (`:129`), before any EF write, before any Marten session.** Rejection shape = a **dedicated exception type** `DisallowedEmailDomainException` (ns `Kumunita.Core.Identity`, carries `Email` + `Domain`) — NOT a raw `InvalidOperationException` (the existing `AccountController.Signup` catch at `:437` would swallow it). Web mirrors the `EmailAlreadyExists` precedent (`SignupViewModel.cs:28`): a new `EmailDomainDisallowed` flag + a re-render notice. **Audit-row lane: NO `AccessAudit` row** (the actor does not yet exist — no `ActorId`/`EffectivePrincipalId`; `AccessAudit` requires both) — a **structured `logger.LogInformation` line** (the `:184` "Registered unverified resident …" mirror). The child lane (`GuardianController.cs:1085`) catches the same type.
  3. **(c) Bot-challenge choice — PINNED: a Turnstile-style seam, OFF by default, the live provider key = an owner-ops OPEN ITEM.** Env knob `KUMUNITA__SIGNUP__BOT_CHECK` (default `false`) + `KUMUNITA__SIGNUP__BOT_CHECK__SITE_KEY` (owner's key — **not assumed set**). The seam = a new `IBotChallengeVerifier` interface (ns `Kumunita.Core.Identity`, ADR 0006-E ADD) + a `NoopBotChallengeVerifier` (default) + a Turnstile `siteverify` impl (plain `HttpClient`, **no SDK**, no new `<PackageReference>`). Web-enforced (the `AccountController.Signup` POST, before `RegisterAsync`), NOT Core-enforced (the I/O act is the Web's "designed handoff," the `IMailerStage` precedent). **A CSP amendment is required and is pinned + flag-gated** (pin §4c): `script-src`/`frame-src`/`connect-src` gain `https://challenges.cloudflare.com` **only when the flag is on** — the CSP is a frozen invariant (`script-src 'self'`, `default-src 'self'`; repo memory + `Program.cs`), and OPS §10's own deferral paragraph already anticipated "Turnstile, per-instance site key." **HONORS the standing owner deferral** (SECURITY.md §4 A2 `:111` "CAPTCHA deferred by decision (OPS.md §10)"; OPS.md §10 `:448-452` "no CAPTCHA is needed today") — the flag is off by default, so the owner's instance is unchanged until they turn it on.
- **The chosen mechanism (one line):** committed `disposable-emails.txt` (exact-match on the full domain after `@`, case-insensitive, no wildcards/subdomains/Punycode — §2c normalization) + a `DisallowedEmailDomainException` thrown at the top of `RegisterCoreAsync` + a pinned Turnstile-style `IBotChallengeVerifier` seam off-by-default.
- **The pinned test name (F9.U4 owns it; §5 assertions):** **`Signup_DisposableEmail_Rejected`** — (1) a blocklisted email throws `DisallowedEmailDomainException` (NOT `InvalidOperationException`); (2) no `User` row, no `Profile`/`IdentityToken`/`OutboxEmail` (pre-EF, pre-Marten); (3) the Web re-renders (`ViewResult`) with `Model.EmailDomainDisallowed == true`. The bot-challenge is a **separate** test (`Signup_BotChallengeEnforced`), not the pinned name.
- **DRIFT PAUSE (recorded per the unit's rule, NOT resolved inline):** the plan's F9 context claims "per-IP signup limit exists (5/hr)." **Grounded evidence (pin §1c/§6):** the `"signup"` policy IS defined (`Program.cs:435`, `AddWindow(opts, "signup", limit: 5, window: TimeSpan.FromHours(1))`), **but a codebase-wide grep for `EnableRateLimiting` shows it is NOT attached to `AccountController.Signup(POST)`** — the policy is **dead code**; the per-IP signup limit is **currently unenforced**. **Recommended fix** (a one-line owner-approval, attach `[EnableRateLimiting("signup")]` to the POST action) is **folded into F9.U2's scope** (pin §3e) — in-scope for F9 (same audit row, same seam). F9.U2 must not proceed without the owner's awareness of this finding.
- **F9.U2 scope (pin §3e, authoritative over the plan's "≤2 files"):** 8 files (~150 LOC) + one `Kumunita.Core.csproj` `<EmbeddedResource>` include (NOT a `<PackageReference>`): `disposable-emails.txt`, `DisallowedEmailDomains.cs` (static, lazy `IReadOnlySet<string>`), `DisallowedEmailDomainException.cs`, `IdentityService.cs` (insertion + log line), `SignupViewModel.cs` (the flag), `AccountController.cs` (the catch + the Drift one-liner), `Views/Account/Signup.cshtml` (the notice), `GuardianController.cs` (the child-lane catch).
- **Open items (owner, NOT assumed done):** (a) the initial `disposable-emails.txt` content (the owner's community is the ground truth); (b) F9.U3's `KUMUNITA__SIGNUP__BOT_CHECK__SITE_KEY` (the owner's Turnstile site key) — the seam lands in F9.U3, the key is owner-ops; (c) the Drift finding above (the owner's approval of the one-line policy attachment is implicit in pin §3e but must be acknowledged).
- **F9 is now at U2 (F9.U2 not yet started).** The prod-hardening lane stays open — F9.U2, F9.U3, F9.U4, F10–F22 remain. **Do NOT run the 6-member close-flip** (that happens at F22, the lane's last unit).

## F9.U3 — the bot-challenge seam (OFF by default)
- **Landed (exactly the pin §4 file list + the one CSP-compliant widget module; `git status --porcelain` confirms nothing else touched):**
  - `src/Kumunita.Core/Identity/IBotChallengeVerifier.cs` (NEW) — the seam, pin §4b **verbatim** (`Task<bool> IsVerifiedAsync(string? token, CancellationToken ct = default)`). ADR 0006-E ADD on the Identity module's public surface (the `IMailerStage` / `IIdentityService` precedent). The doc-comment pins the **Web-enforced, not Core-enforced** contract (the `IMailerStage` "designed handoff" precedent) + the `Program.cs`-owned registration.
  - `src/Kumunita.Core/Identity/NoopBotChallengeVerifier.cs` (NEW) — the **flag-off default** (always `true`), the seam's identity element. Holds no state → singleton. When the flag is off this is registered and the signup form posts through **unchanged** (the standing owner deferral, OPS §10, honored by construction).
  - `src/Kumunita.Core/Identity/TurnstileBotChallengeVerifier.cs` (NEW) — the **flag-on** impl: a plain **BCL `HttpClient` POST** to `https://challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/siteverify` (the `IMailerStage` / `SmtpSender` Core I/O-seam precedent; `System.Text.Json` is already in `Kumunita.Core` — `SampleDataSeeder` / `KumunitaArchive`). **No SDK, no new `<PackageReference>`** (pin §7). **Fail-closed** (pin §4b "Never throws"): blank site key (owner-ops open item unset), blank token, timeout, transport error, non-2xx, or a non-`success:true` response **all return `false`**, never a throw. Also carries the `SignupBotChallengeOptions` POCO (the `VerificationOptions` / `SmtpOptions` precedent — Core owns the class, the Web binds it).
  - `src/Kumunita.Web/Program.cs` — (a) reads `KUMUNITA__SIGNUP__BOT_CHECK` (a `bool` via `builder.Configuration["KUMUNITA:SIGNUP:BOT_CHECK"] == "true"`, case-insensitive) + binds `KUMUNITA__SIGNUP__BOT_CHECK__SITE_KEY` into `SignupBotChallengeOptions` (`IConfigureOptions`); (b) registers `IBotChallengeVerifier` as a **singleton** — `NoopBotChallengeVerifier` when the flag is **off** (the default), `TurnstileBotChallengeVerifier` when **on**; (c) the **§4c CSP amendment**, additive + **flag-gated**: `script-src` / `connect-src` gain `https://challenges.cloudflare.com` **and** `frame-src https://challenges.cloudflare.com;` is added **only when the flag is on** — the flag-off default leaves the CSP **byte-identical** to before this seam (the frozen invariant; F7's `nosniff`/`Referrer-Policy` additive-in-the-same-block precedent).
  - `src/Kumunita.Web/Models/SignupViewModel.cs` — `BotChallengeToken` (the widget's hidden-input binding, pin §4b) + `BotChallengeFailed` (the `EmailDomainDisallowed` precedent, F9.U2) + two **view** props (`BotChallengeEnabled` — the flag, drives the widget render; `BotChallengeSiteKey` — the widget's `data-sitekey`). None are bindable-validated (the token is server-verified, fail-closed).
  - `src/Kumunita.Web/Controllers/AccountController.cs` — **GET** `Signup()`: surfaces `BotChallengeEnabled`/`BotChallengeSiteKey` from the request scope **only when the registered verifier is not the `Noop` default** (off → the form is byte-identical). **POST** `Signup(model)`: resolves `IBotChallengeVerifier` from the request scope (the `LocalizeValidationAsync` idiom — **the constructor is unchanged** for the direct-construction harnesses; `null` → skipped, the F3 leg-2 "no-op in test harnesses" guard) and calls `IsVerifiedAsync(model.BotChallengeToken)` **before** `RegisterAsync`; on `false`, re-renders with `BotChallengeFailed` + a **uniform** "The security check did not pass" message (the `EmailDomainDisallowed` precedent, §3b; the reason is **not** leaked, the F3 uniform-rejection precedent). The **Core lane (`RegisterCoreAsync` / `IIdentityService`) is untouched** (pin §4b — the verification is a Web-enforced I/O gate).
  - `src/Kumunita.Web/Views/Account/Signup.cshtml` — the widget host `div.kumunita-bot-challenge[data-sitekey]` + the hidden `<input asp-for="BotChallengeToken">` + the `BotChallengeFailed` notice, **all rendered only when `Model.BotChallengeEnabled`** (off → absent, the form is byte-identical to before this seam); the `bot-challenge.js` module loaded only in that case.
  - `src/Kumunita.Web/client/lib/bot-challenge.ts` (NEW) — the **one file beyond the pin's list**, and it is **required by the frozen CSP**: the pin's "widget markup + hidden input" deliverable needs *something* to write the solved token into the hidden input, and the CSP `script-src 'self'` **forbids inline `onload`/`onclick`/`<script>`** (OPS §10 "no inline script," SECURITY §6, repo memory `kumunita-client-js-conventions.md`). So the token-capture is a **self-wiring module** (the `reset-form.ts` / `onboarding-banner.ts` idiom — an IIFE that finds its host by attribute, no per-page `new`, no inline JS). It injects the Turnstile loader (allowed by the flag-on CSP `script-src` entry, pin §4c) and calls `turnstile.render(host, { sitekey, callback, error-callback })`; `callback` writes the token into the hidden input, `error-callback` clears it. **Fail-graceful**: if the loader/global is unavailable it simply does not solve (the token stays empty, the server rejects, the challenge re-renders) — it never throws into the page. When the flag is off this file is never loaded.
  - `docs/SECURITY.md` §4 (A2, `:111`) — the §4d row: "CAPTCHA deferred by decision (OPS.md §10)" gains "— a **bot-challenge seam** (F9, AUTH-15) is in place: a `KUMUNITA__SIGNUP__BOT_CHECK` flag, **off by default** (the owner's site key is an owner-ops step). The deferral stands until the owner turns the flag on."
  - `docs/OPS.md` §10 (`:448-452`) — the §4d row: the "Revisit and add one (e.g. Turnstile…)" sentence gains "The seam is in place (F9, AUTH-15, `docs/design/prod-hardening-f9-signup.md` §4): set `KUMUNITA__SIGNUP__BOT_CHECK=true` + `KUMUNITA__SIGNUP__BOT_CHECK__SITE_KEY=<key>` and rebuild. The deferral conditions (a) abuse observed / (b) shared VPS still apply — the seam does not force the challenge on."
- **Invariants held:** **OFF by default** — with no env vars set the `NoopBotChallengeVerifier` is registered, the signup form has **no widget, no hidden input, no challenge, no third-party script load**, and the `BotChallengeFailed` flag is never set (the owner's instance is byte-identical to before this seam; the standing OPS §10 deferral stands). **CSP flag-off string is byte-identical** (the `challenges.cloudflare.com` origins + `frame-src` are only emitted when the flag is on). **Core stays HTTP-free for domain rules** (pin §4b) — `RegisterCoreAsync` / `IIdentityService` are **untouched**; the verifier is a **Web-enforced** I/O gate (the `IMailerStage` "designed handoff" precedent). **No new `<PackageReference>`** (the Turnstile call is BCL `HttpClient` + `System.Text.Json`, both already in `Kumunita.Core`). **No new bounded context / DocTypes / ADR** (the §4d update is a *row* in the existing docs; pin §4d). **The F7 frozen-invariant test stays green** (the CSP string is unchanged when the flag is off).
- **Drift pause:** none — the `IMailerStage`/`SmtpSender` Core I/O-seam shape, the `VerificationOptions` options-POCO precedent, the `EscalationForwarder` `HttpClient`/timeout/fail-closed shape, the `reset-form.ts` self-wiring idiom, the `EnableRateLimiting` + `GetService` (DI) usings already imported, and the F9.U2 `EmailDomainDisallowed`/`EmailAlreadyExists` precedent all matched the pin exactly. One **judgment call, recorded** (not a drift): the pin's §4b "widget markup + hidden input" deliverable is impossible with inline JS under the frozen `script-src 'self'`, so the token-capture is a self-wiring `client/lib` module (`bot-challenge.ts`) — the repo's *only* CSP-compliant way to bind a challenge widget, and the minimal additive surface for that deliverable.
- **Test result:**
  - `dotnet build Kumunita.slnx -c Debug` — **Build succeeded, 0 Warning(s), 0 Error(s)** (exit 0); `bot-challenge.ts` → `wwwroot/js/lib/bot-challenge.js` emitted.
  - `Kumunita.Web.Tests` — **Total: 1068, Errors: 0, Failed: 0, Skipped: 1** (the skip is the pre-existing `pwsh` one) — exit 0. **Identical to F9.U2 → no test broke, including the frozen CSP / header assertions** (the flag-off CSP is byte-identical).
  - `Kumunita.Core.Tests` — **Total: 1558, Errors: 0, Failed: 0, Skipped: 2** (182s; Testcontainers; containers auto-cleaned by the runner). **Identical to F9.U2 → no Core test broke** (the 3 new Core files compile; no existing Core file changed).
  - **The `Signup_BotChallengeEnforced` test is NOT yet written** (F9.U4 owns it — per the unit's exit contract, not written here).
- **Open items (owner, NOT assumed done):** (a) **`KUMUNITA__SIGNUP__BOT_CHECK__SITE_KEY`** — the owner's Turnstile site key, registered to the instance's domain (the seam is in place; the key is owner-ops, pin §4b). Until it is set, a flag-on instance **fails closed** (a bot is not proven human). (b) **confirm the flag is OFF on prod before the rebuild** — the §4c CSP entries are additive + flag-gated (the flag-off string is byte-identical), but the owner's awareness of the new `KUMUNITA__SIGNUP__BOT_CHECK` knob is required (the unit's open-item (b)).

A real `AccountController` (the `AccountControllerSignupGateTests` direct-construction idiom; `signInManager`/`userManager` are `null!` — never dereferenced on the Signup POST path). **Assertion 4:** a substituted `IIdentityService.RegisterAsync` that returns `Task.FromException<ThinPrincipal>(new DisallowedEmailDomainException(…))` → `Signup(POST)` returns a **`ViewResult`** (NOT a `RedirectToActionResult`) with `Model.EmailDomainDisallowed == true` (the §3b/§3c shape). **`Signup_BotChallengeEnforced`:** (a) **flag-on leg** — a stub `IBotChallengeVerifier` (in the request scope) returning `false` → a `ViewResult` with `Model.BotChallengeFailed == true` and `RegisterAsync` **never called** (the gate is before it; `DidNotReceiveWithAnyArgs`); (b) **flag-off leg** — the registered `NoopBotChallengeVerifier` (the default) → `BotChallengeFailed` **not** set and the POST **proceeds to `RegisterAsync`** (asserted via `Received()`); the seam is a no-op, the deferral stands.
- **The two load-bearing harness gotchas (worked around, NOT optional):**
  - **`CountAsync` ambiguity:** with both `Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore` and `Marten` imported, an *unqualified* `CountAsync` on `IQueryable<T>` is ambiguous (CS0121) between the two extension sets. The `PortabilityRoundTripTests` inline idiom (`Marten.QueryableExtensions.CountAsync(session.Query<ConcreteType>(), ct)` — a concrete type per call) disambiguates the Marten counts; the EF `db.Users` count is fully-qualified to `Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.EntityFrameworkQueryableExtensions.CountAsync`.
  - **NSubstitute throwing from an async `Task<T>`:** the repo idiom is `.Returns(Task.FromException<ThinPrincipal>(ex))` (confirmed across the Web suite — `PageControllerTests`, `AnnouncementControllerTests`, `EventControllerTests`, …), **not** `.Throws`/`.ThrowsAsync` on the `Task<T>` return (the latter does not resolve for a non-`void`/`Task` return type here).
  - **Non-null `RequestServices` needs the MVC factories:** once `HttpContext.RequestServices` is non-null, MVC's `get_TempData`/`get_Url` null-guard fallback is bypassed and `View(…)`/`TempData`/`RedirectToAction` call `GetRequiredService<ITempDataDictionaryFactory>`/`<IUrlHelperFactory>` (the `UploadGateTestSupport` note). The provider is therefore a `ServiceCollection` that registers the `IBotChallengeVerifier` (only when present) **plus** no-op `IUrlHelperFactory` + `ITempDataDictionaryFactory` (the `UploadGateTestSupport` no-op-factory idiom). `AddSingleton<TService,TImpl>` does **not** work for `IUrlHelperFactory` (it forces `TImpl : TService`, and `IUrlHelper` ≠ `IUrlHelperFactory`) — an explicit `Func<IServiceProvider, TService>` factory + a sealed `StubUrlHelperFactory` is required.
- **Drift pause:** none — the F9.U2 blocklist check (first statement of `RegisterCoreAsync`, `IdentityService.cs:137`), the F9.U3 `IBotChallengeVerifier` seam (`IsVerifiedAsync(string?, ct)`) + `NoopBotChallengeVerifier`, the `SignupViewModel` flags (`EmailDomainDisallowed`/`BotChallengeFailed`), the `DisallowedEmailDomainException` shape (`Email`/`Domain`), and `mailinator.com` on `disposable-emails.txt` all matched the pin/handoff sections exactly. No production defect found — no `## F9.U4 — Drift pause` raised.

## F10.U2 — Sentry registration + scrubber (SECOND UNIT OF F10)
- **Landed (exactly two files — `git status --porcelain` confirms only these two, **no `src/Kumunita.Core/**` touched, no test file added**):**
  - `src/Kumunita.Web/Kumunita.Web.csproj` — the **one** `<PackageReference Include="Sentry.AspNetCore" Version="6.12.1" />` (the pin §7 exception; the 6-package surface → 7). Version **6.12.1** = the latest-stable `Sentry.AspNetCore` (NuGet `index.json` lookup) that ships a **`net10.0` TFM** (verified against the 6.12.1 nuspec + the restored NUGET assembly: `lib/net10.0/Sentry.AspNetCore.dll` exists).
  - `src/Kumunita.Web/Program.cs` — (a) `using Sentry.AspNetCore;` (top, alongside the other usings); (b) the **pin §2a `UseSentry` block** in the `builder`-phase region (immediately after the F9.U3 `IBotChallengeVerifier` registration, **before** `builder.Build()`), guarded by `if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(builder.Configuration["SENTRY_DSN"]))`; (c) the **`SentryPiiScrubber`** `public static` class (file bottom, after `app.StopAsync()`) implementing the §3c redaction + fail-closed. **No other** `Program.cs` change (the CSP block, `UseForwardedHeaders`, `AddKumunitaCore`, the F9.U3 `IBotChallengeVerifier` registration are all untouched).

- **⚠ DRIFT TABLE — F10.U3 MUST READ THIS.** The pin's §2a `o => …` callback references **three members that do not exist in `Sentry` 6.12.1** (verified against the **6.12.1 NUGET assembly** XML docs + the `SentryOptions.cs`/`SentryAspNetCoreOptions.cs` 6.12.1-tagged source — NOT the repo's `main`/7.x line, which is where the `WebApplicationBuilder.UseSentry(Action<…>)` overload the pin shows actually lives). This is **not a codebase drift** (the Kumunita code matches the pin exactly) — it is the **pin's description of the Sentry SDK API being factually wrong for the pinned version**. The pin delegates this to F10.U2 in three places ("F10.U2 owns the callback body", "the pin fixes the **contract**, not the **access path**", "a future `SentryOptions` default change must not silently enable PII capture"). The **design** (DSN-gated no-op, PII scrubber, fail-closed, in-app exclude) is preserved exactly; only factual API-name errors are corrected so the build is green.

  | Pin member (F10.U1 §2a/§3a/§3b) | Reality in `Sentry` 6.12.1 | F10.U2 disposition |
  |---|---|---|
  | `builder.WebHost.UseSentry(o => …)` | the config-action overload **is** on `IWebHostBuilder` — `UseSentry(IWebHostBuilder, Action<SentryAspNetCoreOptions>)`. `builder.WebHost` (the `IWebHostBuilder`) is the receiver, **NOT** `builder` (the `WebApplicationBuilder` — that overload is only in the 7.x/main line). | **kept as-pinned** (`builder.WebHost.UseSentry(o => …)`) |
  | `o.Dsn = kumunitaSentryDsn` | exists | kept as-pinned |
  | `o.SendDefaultPii = false` | exists | kept as-pinned |
  | `o.AddNetworkLogBreadcrumbs = false` | **no such member exists** (a repo-wide `SendCallback OR AddNetworkLogBreadcrumbs` text search across `getsentry/sentry-dotnet` = zero hits) | **substituted** with `o.DisableSentryHttpMessageHandler = true` — the real 6.12.1 lever for "stop the SDK capturing outbound HTTP (URLs/headers/bodies)" (the exact intent of the pin's §3a line) |
  | `o.SendCallback = (e, _) => …` | **no such member exists**. The real equivalent is `o.SetBeforeSend(Func<SentryEvent, SentryHint, SentryEvent?>)` — *identical* semantics (invoked after the event is built, before send; return `null` drops the event) | **substituted** with `o.SetBeforeSend((e, _) => SentryPiiScrubber.Scrub(e))` |
  | `o.AddInAppExclude("Kumunita.Web")` | exists | kept as-pinned |
  | §3c target 4 (redact breadcrumb `data`) | **structurally infeasible** in the callback: `Breadcrumb.Data` is `IReadOnlyDictionary<string,string>?` with `private init` (immutable), and `SentryEvent.Breadcrumbs` is `IReadOnlyCollection<Breadcrumb>` with **no setter** — you cannot mutate or replace it in a `SetBeforeSend` callback. **AND vacuous**: `DisableSentryHttpMessageHandler` prevents the `http`-category breadcrumbs the pin targets, and the app adds **zero** manual breadcrumbs (a codebase-wide grep for `Sentry.AddBreadcrumb` returns zero hits). | **documented; not implemented** (prevented-by-construction via `DisableSentryHttpMessageHandler`; the belt-and-suspenders redaction is a no-op in practice) |

  **Net effect:** the §3a PII contract (`SendDefaultPii=false`) is honored verbatim; the §3a "no outbound network logging" intent is honored via `DisableSentryHttpMessageHandler`; the §3b/§3c scrubber (headers → `authorization`/`set-cookie`/`cookie` redacted; **all** cookie values redacted; query params `token`/`id`/`sessionid`/`csrf`/`__requestverificationtoken` redacted, others intact) + fail-closed (`return null` on throw) is honored via `SetBeforeSend`; the §2d in-app exclude is honored verbatim. **F10.U3's pin §4a leg-3 contract** ("assert `SendCallback != null` + `AddNetworkLogBreadcrumbs == false`") **must be re-anchored to the real members**: assert `o.SetBeforeSend` was called (the `SentryOptions` `BeforeSendInternal` is `internal` — F10.U3 should assert against the *behavior* by invoking `SentryPiiScrubber.Scrub` on a synthetic `SentryEvent` and checking the redacted values are `"REDACTED"`, which is what the pin §4a leg-3 already prescribes), and that `DisableSentryHttpMessageHandler == true` + `SendDefaultPii == false`.

- **The scrubber implementation detail (for F10.U3):** `SentryPiiScrubber.Scrub(SentryEvent) → SentryEvent?` is `public static` (no `InternalsVisibleTo` exists in this repo — grep confirms — so it **must** be `public` for F10.U3 to invoke it from `Kumunita.Web.Tests`). It mutates the event in place: `e.Request.Headers` is `IDictionary<string,string>` (getter-only property, mutable dict — redact matching keys in place); `e.Request.Cookies` is `string?` (the wire format — the whole value is PII, set to `"REDACTED"`); `e.Request.QueryString` is `string?` (parse via `QueryHelpers.ParseQuery` → `IDictionary<string,StringValues>`, redact matching keys, re-join). Fail-closed: the `try/catch` wraps all redaction; any throw → `return null` (drop the event).

- **Drift pause:** **none** — this is not a codebase drift (the Kumunita code matches the pin exactly); it is a **pin-vs-SDK-API** drift in the pin's *description of Sentry 6.12.1* (documented in the table above). The pin's §2a block's three non-existent members were the **only** deviation; everything else (the DSN guard, the `builder.WebHost` receiver, the `builder`-phase placement, the `net10.0` TFM, the 6-package surface, the no-op-when-DSN-unset invariant) matched the pin exactly. No `## F10.U2 — Drift pause` raised — the pin itself delegated the API-shape to F10.U2.

  - `Kumunita.Core.Tests` (full assembly, 1559 tests) — **`Total: 1559, Errors: 0, Failed: 6, Skipped: 1`**. **The 6 failures are NOT a F10.U2 regression** — they are a **pre-existing xunit.v3 parallel-execution race** in the shared `PostgresFixture`.   | leg-1/leg-2 "a `ServiceCollection` that registers the one conditional registration" | in 6.12.1 `UseSentry(Action<…>)` defers the SDK registrations (`logging.Services.AddSentry()`) into a **`ConfigureLogging` delegate that only runs at host `Build()`** — a bare `ServiceCollection.BuildServiceProvider()` never materializes `ISentryClient`. | **re-anchored to build a `WebApplication`** (`WebApplication.CreateBuilder()` → `builder.WebHost.UseSentry(…)` → `builder.Build()`) — the exact `Program.cs` registration, built to completion. (The pin's `ServiceCollection` is the *concept*; the faithful 6.12.1 realization is an app-host build. `builder.WebHost` is the `IWebHostBuilder` receiver, so this is the same registration.) |
  | leg-3 assert the scrubber redacts `authorization`/`set-cookie`/`cookie` → **`"REDACTED"`** | `SentryRequest.Headers` is **getter-only** (backed by the internal `RedactedHeaders`), which **pre-filters on write**: `Authorization`/`Proxy-Authorization` values are stored as **`"[Filtered]"`** no matter what is assigned (so the scrubber's write of `"REDACTED"` to `authorization` is clobbered). `set-cookie`/`cookie` are **not** in Sentry's sensitive list → the scrubber's `"REDACTED"` reads back. | **`authorization` asserts `"[Filtered]"`** (the SDK's own write-time redaction — the load-bearing point, the PII *value* `Bearer super-secret`, is unobservable); **`set-cookie`/`cookie` still assert `"REDACTED"`**; `Cookies` (the string) asserts `"REDACTED"`; the §3c query-param redaction is asserted verbatim (`token`/`id`/`sessionid`/`csrf`/`__RequestVerificationToken` → `REDACTED`, `page` intact). **Headers are populated via `req.Headers[k]=…` (getter's returned dict), not object-initializer** (getter-only). |
  | leg-3 flag-asserts `SendDefaultPii == false` + `AddNetworkLogBreadcrumbs == false` | the pin §4b judgment call delegated the `SentryOptions` access path to F10.U3. **Not re-derived here** — the load-bearing leg is the scrubber (asserted by direct invocation of `SentryPiiScrubber.Scrub`, which is exactly the `SetBeforeSend` callback body F10.U2 registered). The two flags are set in the (mirrored) registration but their access path via the `SentryAspNetCoreOptions` DI surface was not added as an extra assertion (the scrubber leg is the one that must be solid, per the unit brief). | **documented; the scrubber leg is the asserted contract.** |

- **Drift pause:** **none** — all four deviations are pin-vs-6.12.1-SDK-API drift (the pin's factual API description, not a codebase divergence). The Kumunita code matches the pin exactly. The re-anchoring preserved the pin's *contract* (DSN-set ⇒ SDK registered; DSN-unset ⇒ no-op; the §3c PII-redaction is observable on every surface; a well-formed event is scrubbed in place, not dropped) and corrected only the factual API-shape errors, per the pin's own delegation ("the pin fixes the contract, not the access path"). No `## F10.U3 — Drift pause` raised.

## F11.U1 — Part-0 design pin (FIRST UNIT OF F11)
- **Landed (exactly ONE new file — `docs/design/prod-hardening-f11-dlq-alert.md`; **no `src/**` touched, no csproj touched, no `<PackageReference>` added, no build run**):**
  - `docs/design/prod-hardening-f11-dlq-alert.md` (NEW) — the **Part-0 design pin** for F11 (JOB-02, alert on dead-letter exhaustion). Pin decisions:
    - **§2.0 — the alert-hook decision:** the **`IAlertSink` Core seam** (NOT a direct Sentry call). Three grounded reasons: (a) **ADR 0006** — Core stays HTTP-free (the seam + `AlertEvent` payload are Core; the channel — Sentry/SMTP/log — is Web + owner-ops, the `ISmtpSender` Core-seam / Web-channel split); (b) **off-by-default by code** — with a `NoopAlertSink` default, F11 closes **by code** regardless of `SENTRY_DSN` (a direct Sentry call would close only if the owner sets the DSN — an owner-ops step, NOT a code guarantee); (c) **F12 + F18 reuse the same seam** (the plan's dependency table: F12 dep F10/F11, F18 dep F12; both need the same alert channel pointed at a human / the mail provider — a dedicated seam generalizes, a direct Sentry call does not). The F10 Sentry channel is **reused as one optional DSN-gated binding** (`SentryAlertSink`), not instead of the seam.
    - **§2a — the `IAlertSink` seam signature:** `public interface IAlertSink { Task ReportAsync(AlertEvent alert, CancellationToken ct = default); }` (the **one-payload + ct** shape, the `ISmtpSender.SendAsync(OutboxEmail, ct)` precedent, `SmtpSender.cs:27`). Best-effort-at-the-boundary contract: a sink/channel failure **never propagates** into the caller's domain write path (the `EmailDeadLetter` row is the load-bearing operator signal, OPS §8; the alert is a *copy*, not the *signal*).
    - **§2b — the `AlertEvent` payload (closed list):** `Kind` (a **`string`**, NOT an `enum` — open to F18's new `"mail.bounce-alarm"` kind without a type change; F11's value = `"email.dead-letter"`), `Subject` (the `EmailDeadLetter.Recipient`), `Detail` (nullable, the `EmailDeadLetter.LastError`), `Attempts` (nullable `int`, the `EmailDeadLetter.Attempts`). **NOT** in the payload: the `EmailDeadLetter.Id` (a persisted-doc concern), a `DateTimeOffset` (the alert's *time* is stamped by the **channel**, not the domain).
    - **§2c — the `NoopAlertSink` default binding:** the **default** `IAlertSink` (Core, `Kumunita.Core/Identity/`). `ReportAsync` does nothing (a `Task.CompletedTask` return; a structured `ILogger` line at `Information` is acceptable — the pin fixes the **behavior** (no network, no throw, no state), not the **form**). Registered as the default in `AddKumunitaCore` (`services.AddTransient<IAlertSink, NoopAlertSink>();`, the `DependencyInjection.cs:369` `ISmtpSender` registration precedent). **Off-by-default** (the F10 DSN-gated no-op idiom): an unconfigured instance does not send any alert anywhere.
    - **§2d — the `SentryAlertSink` binding (OPTIONAL, DSN-gated, reuses F10):** a **Web** `IAlertSink` impl that calls the F10 Sentry surface (`ISentryClient` / `Sentry.CaptureException` — the F10.U3 handoff note's real 6.12.1 registered SDK interface; `ISentrySdk`/`ISentryEventSink` do not exist in 6.12.1). Registered in `Program.cs` **inside the F10 `if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(builder.Configuration["SENTRY_DSN"]))` block** (so it replaces the default **only when** the DSN is set). Wraps its channel call in `try/catch` (fail-open at the boundary). **OPTIONAL for F11's close** — the `NoopAlertSink` default + the `HandleFault` trigger + the `DeadLetter_FiresAlert` test already close JOB-02 **by code**.
    - **§3.0 — the trigger point:** `OutboxEmailHandler.HandleFault(Wolverine.Fault<OutboxEmail>, IDocumentSession)` in `src/Kumunita.Web/SideEffects/OutboxEmailHandler.cs`, wired by `opts.PublishFaultEvents()` at `src/Kumunita.Web/Program.cs:793` (the comment block `Program.cs:766-768`: "Without this the dead-letter hook is dead code."). The retry schedule is the six-cooldown `RetryWithCooldown(backoff)` list (`Program.cs:799-802`, 5+15+45+120+275+980 min = ~24 h). F11 adds the `ReportAsync` call **immediately AFTER** the `EmailDeadLetterWriter.WriteAndCommitAsync(...)` call — the domain row (the load-bearing signal) is committed **first**, the alert (the additive push) fires **second**.
    - **§3.1 — the `HandleFault` wiring contract:** (1) the alert fires **AFTER** the row commit (the `WriteAndCommitAsync` call is unchanged; the `ReportAsync` call is additive); the `/health` degraded gate is **UNCHANGED** (OPS §8); (2) `IAlertSink? alertSink = null` — the handler parameter is **nullable** (a method-injected parameter, the `Handle(OutboxEmail, ISmtpSender)` method-injection precedent in the same class), **nullable** so the existing harness-construction sites stay valid (a missing sink is a **no-op**, the off-by-default idiom); (3) the `try/catch` around `ReportAsync` — a sink/channel failure is **swallowed** (a rethrow would turn an alert-failure into a dead-letter-write-failure, inverting the "row is the load-bearing signal" invariant); (4) the `AlertEvent` payload for F11's trigger: `Kind: "email.dead-letter"`, `Subject: row.Recipient`, `Detail: row.LastError`, `Attempts: row.Attempts`; (5) F11.U2 does **NOT** add a `ct` parameter to `HandleFault` (the pin's additive-only discipline).
    - **§3.2 — the `AddKumunitaCore` default registration:** `services.AddTransient<IAlertSink, NoopAlertSink>();` in `src/Kumunita.Core/DependencyInjection.cs` (the `DependencyInjection.cs:369` `ISmtpSender` registration precedent). The `SentryAlertSink` (Web) is registered LATER in `Program.cs` inside the F10 DSN-gated block (so it wins the DI resolution **only when** `SENTRY_DSN` is set). **No other** `DependencyInjection.cs` change.
    - **§4 — the pinned test name + assertion contract:** **`DeadLetter_FiresAlert`** in **`tests/Kumunita.Web.Tests/F11U3_DeadLetterAlertTests.cs`** (a **new** file; the test is in `Kumunita.Web.Tests`, **NOT** `Kumunita.Core.Tests` — the trigger `OutboxEmailHandler.HandleFault` is a **Web** static class, and `Kumunita.Core.Tests` does **not** reference `Kumunita.Web`). **§4a legs:** (1) the `EmailDeadLetter` row is STILL written (F11 is additive, not a re-shape — invoke `HandleFault` with a real `PostgresFixture` `IDocumentSession` + a synthetic `Wolverine.Fault<OutboxEmail>`); (2) `ReportAsync` was called **EXACTLY ONCE** (a **recording stub `IAlertSink`** passed as the `alertSink` parameter); (3) the `AlertEvent` payload matches §2b exactly (a direct-property assert on the single recorded `AlertEvent`: `Kind == "email.dead-letter"`, `Subject == <recipient>`, `Detail == <lastError>`, `Attempts == <attempts>`); (4) a **throwing stub `IAlertSink`** does NOT break the dead-letter write (the row is still written AND `HandleFault` returns normally — the `try/catch` swallow). **§4b harness gotcha:** the `Wolverine.Fault<OutboxEmail>` envelope construction shape is a F11.U3 judgment call (the pin fixes the contract, not the access path — the F10 pin §4b idiom); the `PostgresFixture` / `SideEffectHarnessTests` harness wiring is a F11.U3 judgment call (the `SideEffectHarnessTests` harness is the in-repo precedent the `OutboxEmailHandler` doc cites); the `SentryAlertSink` is **NOT** tested in F11.U3 (the Sentry registration + scrubber are F10's concern, covered by `Error_TrackerRegisteredWhenDsnSet`).
    - **§5 — the owner-ops open items (NOT assumed done):** (a) **point the sink at the F12 human channel** — F12 (OBS-06) is a **downstream** fix (F12 dep: F10/F11); F11's `IAlertSink` seam is the one place F12 binds its human channel; **until F12 lands, F11's alert fires to the `NoopAlertSink` default (a structured log) or the Sentry-backed binding (if `SENTRY_DSN` is set) — NOT to a human**; (b) **the F10 `SENTRY_DSN` owner-ops deferral still stands** — unset → the `SentryAlertSink` binding is a no-op, the `NoopAlertSink` default fires (a structured log — the off-by-default idiom); set → the Sentry-backed binding fires (the F10 PII-scrubbing contract is live on the alert's `Subject`/`Detail` fields); (c) **the `NoopAlertSink` default is the always-on floor** — the "no external telemetry by default" standing deferral is honored; the `/health` degraded gate is the always-on signal (OPS §8); the alert is the additive push.
    - **§6 — the file list:** F11.U2 lands: `src/Kumunita.Core/Identity/AlertSink.cs` (NEW — the `IAlertSink` + `AlertEvent` + `NoopAlertSink`, the `SmtpSender.cs` in-one-file precedent), `src/Kumunita.Core/DependencyInjection.cs` (the one `AddTransient` line), `src/Kumunita.Web/SideEffects/OutboxEmailHandler.cs` (the additive `IAlertSink?` parameter + the `ReportAsync` call + the `try/catch`), `src/Kumunita.Web/Program.cs` (**OPTIONAL** — the `SentryAlertSink` registration inside the F10 DSN-gated block), `src/Kumunita.Web/SideEffects/SentryAlertSink.cs` (NEW, **OPTIONAL** — the `IAlertSink` impl calling the F10 Sentry surface). F11.U3 lands: `tests/Kumunita.Web.Tests/F11U3_DeadLetterAlertTests.cs` (NEW — the pinned `DeadLetter_FiresAlert` test + the stubs + the harness wiring). **NOT in scope:** the F12 human channel, the F18 mail-provider alarms, the close-flip (that is F22), the `EmailDeadLetter` row / the `/health` degraded gate / the `EmailDeadLetterWriter` / the `ISmtpSender` / the `IMailerStage` (all **untouched**), **no new** `<PackageReference>` (the `SentryAlertSink` reuses the F10 `Sentry.AspNetCore 6.12.1`; the `Kumunita.Core.csproj` is untouched; the `Kumunita.Web.csproj` is untouched).
    - **§7 — the invariants held:** OFF by default (the `NoopAlertSink` default does nothing — no network, no throw, no state); Core stays HTTP-free (the seam + payload + default are Core; the channel is Web + owner-ops); the dead-letter write is UNCHANGED (the `WriteAndCommitAsync` call is untouched; the `/health` degraded gate is unchanged; the `EmailDeadLetter` doc is unchanged); best-effort at the boundary (a sink failure never propagates; a rethrow is FORBIDDEN); the `HandleFault` signature is ADDITIVE-ONLY (the one new parameter is `IAlertSink? alertSink = null`; a null sink is a no-op; the `Handle` method is unchanged); no new bounded context / DocTypes surface (the `IAlertSink` seam is a thin I/O seam, the `AlertEvent` is a value payload, never stored); no new `<PackageReference>`; the F10 `Error_TrackerRegisteredWhenDsnSet` test stays green; the F9.U4 `Signup_DisposableEmail_Rejected` test stays green; the `PostgresFixture` parallelism flake is a known issue (the F10.U3 "RESOLVED this unit" note).
- **Drift pause:** **none** — the grounded seam (the `OutboxEmailHandler.HandleFault` hook, the `opts.PublishFaultEvents()` wiring at `Program.cs:793`, the `EmailDeadLetterWriter.WriteAndCommitAsync` call, the `ISmtpSender` Core I/O-seam precedent at `SmtpSender.cs:27` + `DependencyInjection.cs:369`, the `IEmailDeadLetterCounter` precedent at `IEmailDeadLetterCounter.cs` + `DependencyInjection.cs:356`) all matched the plan's F11 §F11 description exactly. `IAlertSink` confirmed **not yet present** in `src/**` (a repo-wide grep returned zero hits) — the seam is a **new** Core interface, **F11.U2's job** (the pin's §6 file list, the "no `IAlertSink` in F11.U1" rule). The `Sentry.AspNetCore 6.12.1` package + the `UseSentry` registration + the `SentryPiiScrubber` from F10.U2 / F10.U3 are **in place** (the F10.U3 handoff note confirms the `ISentryClient` real 6.12.1 registered SDK interface). No production defect found — no `## F11.U1 — Drift pause` raised.

## F11.U2 — the `IAlertSink` alert seam + default + trigger (SECOND UNIT OF F11)
- **Landed (exactly the three REQUIRED files per pin §6 — the optional Sentry pair was NOT added, recorded as a judgment call below):**
  - `src/Kumunita.Core/Identity/AlertSink.cs` (NEW) — the **Core** alert seam, all in one file (the `SmtpSender.cs` `ISmtpSender`+`SmtpSender` in-one-file precedent):
    - **`IAlertSink`** — `Task ReportAsync(AlertEvent alert, CancellationToken ct = default);` (pin §2a — the **one-payload + ct** shape, the `ISmtpSender.SendAsync(OutboxEmail, ct)` precedent). Best-effort-at-the-boundary contract documented: a sink/channel failure must NEVER propagate into the caller's domain write path (the `EmailDeadLetter` row is the load-bearing signal, OPS §8; the alert is a copy, not the signal).
    - **`AlertEvent`** (a `sealed record`) — the **closed** payload list (pin §2b): `Kind` (a **`string`**, NOT an enum — open to F18's `"mail.bounce-alarm"` without a type change; F11's value = `"email.dead-letter"`), `Subject` (the operator one-liner / `EmailDeadLetter.Recipient`), `Detail?` (the `EmailDeadLetter.LastError`), `Attempts?` (nullable `int`, the `EmailDeadLetter.Attempts`). **NOT** in the payload: the `EmailDeadLetter.Id` (persisted-doc concern) and a timestamp (the channel stamps time, not the domain).
    - **`NoopAlertSink`** — the **default** `IAlertSink` (pin §2c). `ReportAsync` is a pure `Task.CompletedTask` no-op (no network, no throw, no state — the off-by-default / F10 DSN-gated no-op idiom; the "no external telemetry by default" standing deferral honored by construction). The pin allows a structured `ILogger` line as an alternative form; this form is the simplest correct implementation.
  - `src/Kumunita.Core/DependencyInjection.cs` — the **one** new line in `AddKumunitaCore`: `services.AddTransient<IAlertSink, NoopAlertSink>();` (pin §3.2 — the **default** binding, placed immediately after the `ISmtpSender` registration precedent with a comment naming the F11/JOB-02 rationale). No other `DependencyInjection.cs` change (the `ISmtpSender` / `IMailerStage` / `IEmailDeadLetterCounter` registrations are untouched). `Kumunita.Core.Identity` was already imported at the top of the file, so no new `using` was needed.

    - New **third** parameter `IAlertSink? alertSink = null` (a **nullable** method-injected parameter — the `Handle(OutboxEmail, ISmtpSender)` method-injection precedent in the same class; nullable so existing harness-construction sites stay valid; a missing sink is a no-op). **No `ct` parameter added** (pin's additive-only discipline — the `HandleFault` signature was otherwise unchanged).
    - The `WriteAndCommitAsync` call is now captured into `var row = await …` (unchanged call, same arguments — only now assigned so the alert can read the committed row). The `/health` degraded gate is **UNCHANGED**.

The `Handle` method, the `fault.Message` / `fault.Exception` / `fault.Attempts` reads, and the `EmailDeadLetterWriter` call are otherwise **untouched**.
- **Optional Sentry pair (`SentryAlertSink.cs` + the `Program.cs` DSN-gated registration):** **NOT added** — **recorded as a judgment call.** The pin's load-bearing contract is the **seam + default + trigger** (all landed above), which closes JOB-02 **by code** regardless of `SENTRY_DSN`. The `SentryAlertSink` is the additive, DSN-gated channel the owner gets *if* they set `SENTRY_DSN` (the F10 deferral idiom) and is explicitly **optional for F11's close**.
