## F11.U3 — the `DeadLetter_FiresAlert` pinned test (THIRD + FINAL UNIT OF F11)
- **Landed (exactly ONE new file — test-only, no `src/**`, no csproj, no `<PackageReference>`):**
  - `tests/Kumunita.Web.Tests/F11U3_DeadLetterAlertTests.cs` (NEW) — the pinned `DeadLetter_FiresAlert` (pin §4a legs 1–4), driving the **real** `OutboxEmailHandler.HandleFault(fault, session, alertSink)` with a **real** `PostgresFixture` `IDocumentSession` + a **synthetic** `Wolverine.Fault<OutboxEmail>` envelope. Two inline stubs: `RecordingAlertSink : IAlertSink` (appends each `AlertEvent` to a `List<AlertEvent>`, leg-2/leg-3) and `ThrowingAlertSink : IAlertSink` (leg-4, tracks `AttemptCount`).
- **Pins relied on:** F11.U1 design pin §4a (the four legs), §4b (the §4b "pin fixes the contract, not the access path" idiom — the `Fault<T>` envelope construction is the implementation), §2a/§2b (the `IAlertSink` best-effort contract + the closed `AlertEvent` payload), §3 (the `HandleFault` trigger). F11.U2 (the seam + `NoopAlertSink` default + the `HandleFault` trigger it drives). `F5U4_KillAllTests` (the `PostgresFixture` + `IClassFixture` + real-`IDocumentSession` idiom mirrored).
- **The four legs (pin §4a), all green:**
  1. **leg-1** — the `EmailDeadLetter` row is **still** written: `session.Query<EmailDeadLetter>().CountAsync()` went up by exactly **1** (a fresh scratch DB starts at 0). F11 is additive — the row is the load-bearing `/health` degraded signal, unchanged.
  2. **leg-2** — `ReportAsync` was called **exactly once** via the `RecordingAlertSink` (`Assert.Single(sink.Events)` — xUnit2013 over `Assert.Equal(1, count)`).
  3. **leg-3** — the recorded `AlertEvent` matches pin §2b **exactly** (direct-property asserts): `Kind == "email.dead-letter"`, `Subject == <recipient>` (`f11u3-resident@examplium.com`), `Detail == <lastError>` (`"SMTP timeout talking to mail.example:587 (attempt 6 of 6)"`), `Attempts == 6`.
  4. **leg-4 (load-bearing)** — the `ThrowingAlertSink` (whose `ReportAsync` throws) does **not** break the write: the row is **still** written (count +1) AND `HandleFault` **returns normally** (no exception propagates — the `try/catch` swallow, pin §3.1). Also asserted `sink.AttemptCount == 1` (the firing path reached the sink before it threw; the throw was swallowed upstream).
- **The `Wolverine.Fault<OutboxEmail>` construction shape (the §4b access-path judgment call — confirmed by reflection against the 6.50.2 assembly, NOT guessed):**
  - **Single public constructor** (the pin's §1 "the exact `Message` / `Exception` / `Attempts` / `CorrelationId` / `SourceContext` members" is the *concept*; the real 6.50.2 signature is 9-arg): `Fault<T>(T Message, ExceptionInfo Exception, int Attempts, DateTimeOffset FailedAt, string CorrelationId, Guid ConversationId, string TenantId, string Source, IReadOnlyDictionary<string,string?> Headers)`. `Message` / `Exception` / `Attempts` are the only ones `HandleFault` reads (the rest are filled with sensible no-ops: `FailedAt = UtcNow`, `CorrelationId = Guid "N"`, `ConversationId = Guid.NewGuid()`, `TenantId = null` (single-tenant), `Source = "test"`, `Headers = empty dict`).
  - **Two 6.50.2 facts that shape it** (both verified by reflection against the 6.50.2 assembly — a throwaway `.tmp/faultref` probe, deleted + `Kumunita.slnx` reverted after):
    - **`Headers` is `IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string?>`** (nullable value type). A plain `Dictionary<string, string>` is a `CS8620` under `<Nullable>enable</Nullable>` — the test project has `Nullable=enable`. Built as `Dictionary<string, string?>`.
    - **`ExceptionInfo.From(ex, bool1, bool2)` — the FIRST boolean is the include-`Message` flag.** `From(ex, false, false)` yields an **EMPTY** `.Message` (type/stack-trace only); `From(ex, true, true)` preserves it. Leg-3 asserts `AlertEvent.Detail == row.LastError == fault.Exception.Message`, and the `EmailDeadLetterWriter` records `row.LastError = fault.Exception?.Message` — so the envelope is built with `Wolverine.ExceptionInfo.From(new InvalidOperationException(LastError), true, true)` to keep the exact last-delivery-failure string observable end-to-end (the OPS §7 re-queue input). **This is the load-bearing leg-3 input** — a `(ex, false, false)` build would assert `Detail == ""` (technically passing, but not exercising the real last-failure string the pin's §2b names).
- **The `PostgresFixture` wiring (mirrors `F5U4_KillAllTests`, trimmed to Marten):** a fresh scratch DB (per the `PostgresFixture.NewDatabaseAsync` seam), a `DocumentStore` over the `mt` schema with `CoreDocTypes.Configure` (which registers `EmailDeadLetter` — `CoreDocTypes.cs:109`), `ApplyAllConfiguredChangesToDatabaseAsync`, then a real `IDocumentSession` via `store.OpenSession(new Marten.Services.SessionOptions())` (the `SideEffectHarnessTests` precedent — `OpenSession()` alone is a `CS7036` in this Marten version; `SessionOptions` is a required param). **F5U4's EF `Identity` boot is omitted** — `HandleFault` is Wolverine-free / Marten-only (it writes exactly one `EmailDeadLetter` row) and does not touch the `User` table; the EF surface is load-bearing for `UserManager` stamp rotation in F5U4, which has no analog here. (`BootStoreAsync` is an **instance** method so it can read the primary-constructor `fixture` — a static method would be a `CS9105` under C#12.)
- **`NoopAlertSink`-as-default implicitly covered by the null-sink no-op leg:** **yes, implicitly.** The `HandleFault` signature is `IAlertSink? alertSink = null` (the F11.U2 nullable-default, the off-by-default idiom). The existing harness-construction sites (e.g. the `SideEffectHarnessTests` shape, and any future test that calls `HandleFault(fault, session)` with only the session) pass a **null** sink, which is a **no-op** (`if (alertSink is not null) { … }` — the `try/catch` block is skipped). This is the `NoopAlertSink` default's behavioral contract (no network, no throw, no state — the F10 DSN-gated no-op idiom) realized by construction. Leg-4's throwing sink proves the **guard + swallow** path is what protects the write (a null sink is the same guard, short-circuited); the DI default binding (`services.AddTransient<IAlertSink, NoopAlertSink>()` in `AddKumunitaCore`, F11.U2) is the production wiring that resolves to that no-op when no other `IAlertSink` is registered. **No separate "null-sink no-op" leg is asserted** (pin §4a does not require one — the four legs are the contract), but the null-guard is **load-bearing** and is what leg-4's `try/catch` + the `is not null` check together protect.
- **Drift pause:** **none** — the `HandleFault` signature matched the pin exactly (`fault, session, IAlertSink? = null`; the F11.U2 landing). The `EmailDeadLetter` doc matched the pin §2b exactly (`Recipient` / `LastError?` / `Attempts`). The `IAlertSink` / `AlertEvent` / `NoopAlertSink` seam (F11.U2) matched the pin §2a/§2b/§2c exactly. **No production defect found** — no `## F11.U3 — Drift pause` raised.
- **Test result (exit contract):** **build green + both test suites green.** `dotnet build Kumunita.slnx -c Debug` → **Build succeeded. 0 Warning(s) 0 Error(s)** (15 pre-existing warnings, none from this unit). `dotnet exec tests/Kumunita.Web.Tests/bin/Debug/net10.0/Kumunita.Web.Tests.dll` → **Total: 1072, Errors: 0, Failed: 0, Skipped: 1** (up from F11.U2's 1071, **+1** = this test). `dotnet exec tests/Kumunita.Core.Tests/bin/Debug/net10.0/Kumunita.Core.Tests.dll` → **Total: 1559, Errors: 0, Failed: 0, Skipped: 2** (regression check; this unit is test-only in Web.Tests; ~171 s on this machine — machine-speed note, not a regression; `docker container prune -f` run after).
- **Open items (owner, NOT assumed done — carried from F11.U2, unchanged):** (a) **point the sink at the F12 human channel** — F12 (OBS-06) is a **downstream** fix (F12 dep: F10/F11); the `IAlertSink` seam is the one place F12 binds its human channel; **until F12 lands, F11's alert fires to the `NoopAlertSink` default (a no-op) — NOT to a human**. (b) **the F10 `SENTRY_DSN` owner-ops deferral still stands** — the `SentryAlertSink` binding was **not** added in U2 (judgment call); if added later it will be DSN-gated (unset → no-op, the `NoopAlertSink` default fires). (c) **the `NoopAlertSink` default is the always-on floor** — the "no external telemetry by default" standing deferral is honored; the `/health` degraded gate is the always-on signal (OPS §8); the alert is the additive push. The owner's awareness of this split (row = signal, alert = push) is required.
- **F11 is now CODE-COMPLETE (F11.U1 + U2 + U3 all landed — the pin + the seam/default/trigger + the pinned test).** The prod-hardening lane stays **open** — **F12–F22 remain**. **No 6-member close-flip** (that happens at F22, the lane's last unit). Next unit = **F12** (OBS-06, route alerts to a human — the `IAlertSink` F12 binds its channel) — a fresh clean-slate agent.

## F12.U1 — Part-0 design pin (FIRST UNIT OF F12)
- **Landed (exactly ONE new file — `docs/design/prod-hardening-f12-alerts.md`; **no `src/**` touched, no csproj touched, no `<PackageReference>` added, no build run**):**
  - `docs/design/prod-hardening-f12-alerts.md` (NEW) — the **Part-0 design pin** for F12 (OBS-06, route alerts to a human). Pin decisions:
    - **§2.0 — the channel choice:** **option (b) — the `SupportEmail` SMTP channel** (NOT the Sentry channel, option (a)). **Load-bearing reason:** F12's purpose is "route alerts to **a human**" (OBS-06). The `CommunityOptions.SupportEmail` (the seeder's resident-facing support mailbox, `CommunityOptions:16`) is a real, in-repo, non-external destination that exists in **every functioning deployment** → it reaches a human by default in practice. The Sentry channel (a) reaches a **dashboard** (a third-party service the operator must configure + look at), **not** a human — and it *is* the external telemetry the F10 deferral already covers. So (a) does **not** close OBS-06; (b) does. (b) also reuses the **existing Core `ISmtpSender`** (the F10 `ISmtpSender` Core I/O-seam / Web transport split — **no new seam, no new package**), and is the channel **F18** (MAIL-06, dep F12) reuses (kind-agnostic routing, no new channel).
    - **§2a — the F12 channel shape:** `SupportEmailAlertSink`, namespace **`Kumunita.Web.SideEffects`** (the `OutboxEmailHandler` location precedent). Constructor: `ISmtpSender sender, IOptions<CommunityOptions> community, ILogger<SupportEmailAlertSink> logger` (all Core/BCL — ADR 0006, the channel is Web, its deps are Core). `ReportAsync`: compose **one** `OutboxEmail` from the `AlertEvent` (§2b), **guard** on `community.Value.SupportEmail` non-empty (else `Task.CompletedTask` no-op — belt-and-suspenders over the registration gate), **call** `sender.SendAsync(email, ct)`, **wrap in `try/catch`** (the `IAlertSink` best-effort-at-the-boundary contract, `AlertSink.cs` doc; a send failure is **swallowed**, a rethrow is FORBIDDEN).
    - **§2b — the `AlertEvent` → `OutboxEmail` map:** `Recipient = community.Value.SupportEmail` (the **human**); `Subject` = `Kind`+`Subject` (operator one-liner); `Body` = `Detail`+`Attempts` (the OPS §7 re-queue input); `IdempotencyKey` = non-empty (the `SendAsync`/`Handle` guard, `SmtpSender.cs:265`); `Id` = `Guid "N"`; `QueuedAt` = `UtcNow`. The exact strings are **U2 judgment calls** (the pin fixes the contract, not the format).
    - **§2c — the registration point:** a **NEW gated block in `Program.cs`**, placed **after** `AddKumunitaCore()` (line 322) — the F10 U2 / F9 U3 / M25 registration region — `if (SupportEmail non-empty) { services.AddTransient<IAlertSink, SupportEmailAlertSink>(); }`. The gate is the community's own `SupportEmail` (**not** an owner-ops external DSN — met in every real deployment; unmet in dev/scratch → `NoopAlertSink` default fires → no-op, the off-by-default floor). **No other `Program.cs` change** (the F10 Sentry block, F9 bot-check, M25 `UploadGate`, all existing registrations **untouched**). **No other `DependencyInjection.cs` change** (the `NoopAlertSink` default at `:380` is **kept** as-is).
    - **§2d — the DI ordering (the load-bearing leg of F12.U3):** `AddKumunitaCore` registers `IAlertSink → NoopAlertSink` (Core default, `:380`); **then** `Program.cs` (the F12 block, after line 322) registers `IAlertSink → SupportEmailAlertSink`. The F12 binding is registered **LATER → it wins DI resolution** (the F10.U2 / F11 registration-ordering precedent, verbatim: "the Web registration comes after `AddKumunitaCore`, so it wins the resolution"). **F12.U3's load-bearing leg is this DI-ordering assert** (F12 binding wins over `NoopAlertSink`) — a **DI-ordering assert, not a behavior assert** (the behavior is F11.U3's `DeadLetter_FiresAlert`).
    - **§3 — the `AlertEvent` routing contract:** F12 routes **EVERY** `AlertEvent` kind to the configured channel (the `SupportEmailAlertSink` is **kind-agnostic** — it composes an `OutboxEmail` from the `AlertEvent` fields, it does **not** switch on `Kind`; F11's `"email.dead-letter"` rides it today, F18's future `"mail.bounce-alarm"` rides the **same** binding). The `IAlertSink` interface is **UNCHANGED** (no new method, no signature change, no contract change). The `AlertEvent` payload is **UNCHANGED** (F11's closed list; F12's kind-agnostic routing is **why** `Kind` is a `string`, not an `enum`). The `NoopAlertSink` default is **KEPT** (the off-by-default floor; the dev/scratch-instance binding). The `/health` degraded gate is **UNCHANGED** (OPS §8 — F12 is additive, a push alongside the poll). The `HandleFault` trigger is **UNCHANGED** (F11's — F12 changes **what resolves for `IAlertSink`** (the DI binding), **not** the trigger; the production wiring is the Web host's DI container, the F11.U3 in-process test harness injects its own stub).
    - **§4 — the pinned test name + assertion contract:** **`Alert_RoutedToConfiguredChannel`** in **`tests/Kumunita.Web.Tests/`** (a **new** file; the F12 channel is a **Web** class, `Kumunita.Core.Tests` does **not** reference `Kumunita.Web` — the F11.U3 precedent). **§4a legs:** (1) **the DI-ordering leg (load-bearing)** — with the F12 channel registered (gate set), resolving `IAlertSink` yields the **F12 binding** (a recording stub), **NOT** the `NoopAlertSink` (a `Assert.IsType<RecordingAlertSink>` / `Assert.IsNotType<NoopAlertSink>` shape — **U3 judgment call**, the pin fixes the contract: F12 binding wins); (2) **the routing leg (mirroring F11.U3 leg-2/leg-3)** — an `AlertEvent` (the same `"email.dead-letter"` payload F11.U3 leg-3 asserts) is fired; the recording sink **received it** (the fields match — the routing works); (3) **the off-by-default floor (the §3 invariant)** — with the gate **unset** (`SupportEmail` unconfigured), resolving `IAlertSink` yields the **`NoopAlertSink`** default (the floor is **kept**). **The load-bearing leg is leg-1** (the F12 binding **wins** over the `NoopAlertSink` default — the routing works because the DI ordering puts the F12 channel first). **§4b harness gotcha:** how the DI container is built for the leg-1/leg-3 asserts (a **hand-rolled** `ServiceCollection` with the two `AddTransient` calls in the **correct order** — the `NoopAlertSink` default **first** (the `AddKumunitaCore` shape), the F12 binding **second** (the `Program.cs` shape)) is a **U3 judgment call** (the F10 pin §4b / F11 pin §4b "pin fixes the contract, not the access path" idiom); the `RecordingAlertSink` stub is the **same** one F11.U3 used (`F11U3_DeadLetterAlertTests.cs` — the F12 test **reuses** it, not re-derives it); the `SupportEmailAlertSink` real class is **NOT** tested in F12.U3 (its `ISmtpSender.SendAsync` call is the F11 "Core seam, Web channel" split — the real SMTP send is an **owner-ops** concern (pin §7), not a unit-test concern; the DI-ordering + routing contract is the F12 test's job, covered by the recording stub).
    - **§5 — the file list:** F12.U1: `docs/design/prod-hardening-f12-alerts.md` (this pin). F12.U2 (≤2 files, Web): `src/Kumunita.Web/SideEffects/SupportEmailAlertSink.cs` (NEW — the `IAlertSink` impl, the `SmtpSender.cs` / `OutboxEmailHandler.cs` Web-project / Core-seam precedent), `src/Kumunita.Web/Program.cs` (the one new gated registration block, after line 322). **NOT in scope (U2):** the `NoopAlertSink` default (KEPT, `:380`, **untouched**), the `IAlertSink` interface (UNCHANGED), the `AlertEvent` payload (UNCHANGED), the `HandleFault` trigger (UNCHANGED, F11's), the `EmailDeadLetter` row / the `/health` degraded gate (UNCHANGED), **no new** `<PackageReference>` (MailKit is already a Core dependency; `Kumunita.Core.csproj` + `Kumunita.Web.csproj` **untouched**), **no** new bounded context / DocTypes surface. F12.U3 (≤1 file, Web.Tests): `tests/Kumunita.Web.Tests/F12U3_AlertRoutedToChannelTests.cs` (NEW — the pinned `Alert_RoutedToConfiguredChannel`, legs 1–3, the `RecordingAlertSink` stub **reused** from F11.U3, the **real** `NoopAlertSink` for the leg-3 assert).
    - **§7 — the owner-ops open items (NOT assumed done):** (a) **confirm a real recipient is configured on prod** — the F12 channel routes to `CommunityOptions.SupportEmail`; the owner must confirm the **prod** `SupportEmail` is set to a **real, monitored** address (a mailbox a human actually reads) — otherwise the gate is met (the seeder sets it) but the **destination** is not monitored (OBS-06 still not effectively closed); if the owner has **no** support mailbox on prod, the gate is **unmet** → the `NoopAlertSink` default fires → a no-op (the `/health` degraded gate is the only signal, OBS-06 **not** closed — the owner must **either** set a real `SupportEmail` **or** accept the poll-only posture and record it). (b) **the F10 `SENTRY_DSN` deferral STILL STANDS (option (a) not chosen)** — the pin chose (b), **NOT** the Sentry channel; the F10 `SENTRY_DSN` owner-ops deferral is **unchanged by F12** (a **standing deferral**: unset → the F10 Sentry block is a no-op, the pipeline is byte-identical to before the seam); if the owner later wants the Sentry channel too (option (a), a **second** binding, DSN-gated), it is a **separate, additive** unit (register the `SentryAlertSink` inside the F10 DSN-gated block — the F11.U1 §2d design, which F11.U2 deliberately did **not** land); it is **NOT** part of F12 (F12 = the **human** channel / the `SupportEmail`; F10 = the **external** Sentry dashboard / the `SENTRY_DSN`). The owner's **awareness** of this split is required (the F10.U2 / F11.U3 open-item (b) idiom).
    - **§8 — the invariants held:** OFF by default (the `NoopAlertSink` floor is **kept** — no network, no throw, no state for an unconfigured instance; the F12 channel is the **additive push**, not a replacement of the floor); Core stays HTTP-free (ADR 0006 — the seam + payload are Core / F11's / **untouched**; the channel is a **Web** class depending on the **Core** `ISmtpSender` / `CommunityOptions` — the `ISmtpSender` Core I/O-seam / Web transport split); the `IAlertSink` interface is UNCHANGED; the `AlertEvent` payload is UNCHANGED; the `HandleFault` trigger is UNCHANGED; the `/health` degraded gate is UNCHANGED; best-effort at the boundary (the `SupportEmailAlertSink`'s `SendAsync` call is wrapped in `try/catch`; a rethrow is FORBIDDEN); no new `<PackageReference>`; no new bounded context / DocTypes surface; no migration, no `.slnx` change, no `.cs` change in U1; the F10 `Error_TrackerRegisteredWhenDsnSet` test stays green (the F10 Sentry block is **untouched**); the F11.U3 `DeadLetter_FiresAlert` test stays green (the `HandleFault` trigger + the `NoopAlertSink` default are **untouched**); the F9.U4 `Signup_DisposableEmail_Rejected` test stays green (the F9 bot-check block is **untouched**).
- **Drift pause:** **none** — the `IAlertSink` / `AlertEvent` / `NoopAlertSink` seam (`src/Kumunita.Core/Identity/AlertSink.cs`, F11.U2) matched the plan's F12 §F12 description **and** the F11 pin §2a/§2b/§2c **exactly**: `public interface IAlertSink { Task ReportAsync(AlertEvent alert, CancellationToken ct = default); }`, `public sealed record AlertEvent(string Kind, string Subject, string? Detail, int? Attempts);`, `public sealed class NoopAlertSink : IAlertSink` (pure `Task.CompletedTask` no-op). The `NoopAlertSink` default registration (`DependencyInjection.cs:380`, `services.AddTransient<IAlertSink, NoopAlertSink>();`) matched. The `HandleFault` trigger (`OutboxEmailHandler.cs`, the `IAlertSink? alertSink = null` parameter + the `if (alertSink is not null) { try { ReportAsync } catch { } }` block) matched the F11.U2 landing exactly. The `ISmtpSender` Core I/O-seam (`SmtpSender.cs:27`, `Task SendAsync(OutboxEmail email, CancellationToken ct = default)`; `SmtpSender.cs:265` the `Recipient` non-empty guard) matched. The `CommunityOptions.SupportEmail` seam (`CommunityOptions:16`, `public string? SupportEmail { get; set; }`) matched. - **Test result:** **N/A — this is a design-pin unit; the exit contract is NO build, NO test run** (the F11.U1 / F10.U1 / F9.U1 design-pin unit exit-contract idiom). ## F13.U1 — `security.txt` route + VDP text (DESIGN-PIN + IMPLEMENTATION, FIRST UNIT OF F13)
- **Landed (exactly TWO files, Web + docs; **no `src/Kumunita.Core/**` change, no `<PackageReference>`, no migration, no `.slnx` change**):**
  - `docs/SECURITY-VDP.md` (**NEW**, ~110 lines) — the Vulnerability Disclosure Policy **prose**: the stable `Contact` channel (pointing at the `Contact` field in `security.txt` — that field is the authoritative, always-current address), a `Policy` reference, a short **scope** description (the Kumunita platform — self-hosted, single-community — in-scope web/identity/content/media/mail surface + the out-of-scope carve-outs), a **response-time commitment** ("acknowledge within **72 h**", triage within 7 days, best-efforts time-bound fix), and a **safe-harbor / no-reprisal** line (good-faith private testing → no legal claim, no suppression; excludes non-disclosure abuse). Consistent with the privacy-first model in `SECURITY.md` and the F12 `SupportEmail` contact channel.
  - `src/Kumunita.Web/Controllers/WellKnownController.cs` (**NEW**, ~130 lines) — the `GET /.well-known/security.txt` route. **One anonymous action** (`SecurityTxt()`) in a `WellKnownController : Controller`, DI dependency **only** `IOptions<CommunityOptions>` (a Core type — ADR 0006, Web→Core). **No auth, no new seam.**
- **Routing mechanism (U1 judgment call, pinned here):** a **controller action** (`[HttpGet("/.well-known/security.txt")]`), matching the repo idiom (`StaticPagesController` absolute-route precedent, e.g. `[HttpGet("/terms")]`) rather than `app.MapGet` — the app pipeline in `Program.cs` is MVC (`MapControllerRoute` default + the F9/F10/F12 gated registration blocks live in the `AddKumunitaCore` region, not a minimal-API `MapGet` region; there is **no** `app.MapGet` in `Program.cs` today, so a controller is the consistent seam). The body is **built inline** (no file read at request time, no startup-file-read dependency, no `wwwroot/.well-known/` file that a deployer could shadow — `wwwroot/.well-known/` does not exist in the repo): the VDP doc (`SECURITY-VDP.md`) is the canonical **prose** for humans/the repo, and this route carries the RFC 9116 **fields** — one source of truth per artifact, self-contained and trivially testable.
- **RFC 9116 fields included (the pin contract):** `Contact: mailto:<CommunityOptions.SupportEmail>` (the instance's monitored support mailbox — the **F12 channel**; config-driven so it is always the live owner-monitored address, with a stable `mailto:security@localhost` floor only when unconfigured so the field is **never** blank — RFC 9116 requires `Contact`), `Expires: 2027-12-01` (a **future** date, ~13 months out, ≥ the plan's "at least 12 months out"), `Preferred-Languages: en`, `Policy: {scheme}://{host}/.well-known/security.txt` (built from `Request.Scheme`/`Request.Host` — the plan allows "this same security.txt or a dedicated page"; there is no separate served VDP page, so it points at this same route), `Hints: <short in-scope-surface description>`. Response = `Content(body, "text/plain")` → **200** with `text/plain`.
- **Pinned test name (F13.U2 — the test itself is NOT in this unit):** `SecurityTxt_ReturnsValidFields` in `tests/Kumunita.Web.Tests/` (the F13.U2 unit, a Web.Tests file; the F12.U3 / F9.U4 / F10.U3 "Web class → Web.Tests" precedent). **U2 should assert:** the route returns **200** + `text/plain`, and the body contains `Contact:` (non-empty `mailto:`), `Expires:` (a **future** date ≥ 12 months out), `Policy:` (a full URL ending `/.well-known/security.txt`), `Preferred-Languages: en`, and `Hints:` (non-empty). The harness idiom is a **U2 judgment call** (the F10/F12 "pin fixes the contract, not the access path" idiom) — e.g. a hand-rolled `WellKnownController` with a stubbed `IOptions<CommunityOptions>` + a stubbed `Request` (the F3.U5 / KioskControllerTests `ActionContext`/`UrlHelper` stub precedent), then assert on the `ContentResult`.
- **Drift pause:** **none** — the `Program.cs` app pipeline matched the plan's description (MVC `MapControllerRoute` default, the F7 CSP/X-Frame-Options header middleware at `Program.cs:865-899`, the F9/F10/F12 gated registration blocks at `Program.cs:322-400`); the routing idiom (controllers, no `app.MapGet`) was confirmed; `CommunityOptions.SupportEmail` (`CommunityOptions.cs:16`, nullable, `SectionName = "Community"`) matched the F12 pin §2c seam exactly; there is **no** `wwwroot/.well-known/` file and **no** existing `WellKnownController`. **No production defect found** — no `## F13.U1 — Drift pause` raised.
- **Test result:** **N/A — the test is F13.U2** (this unit only pins the name `SecurityTxt_ReturnsValidFields`). **Exit contract met:** `dotnet build Kumunita.slnx -c Debug` → **Build succeeded, 0 Error(s)** (47 warnings, all **pre-existing** — the `Program.cs:1187` CS8604 / xunit analyzers / `Kumunita.Core` CS8603/CS8714 / `Kumunita.Web` view warnings; **none** from the two F13.U1 files). **F13.U1 does NOT run the test dlls** (the repo-documented exit contract for a design-pin + implementation unit; the build is the gate).
- **Open items (owner, NOT assumed done):** **none** — F13 is pure `code` (no owner-ops step). The **one** owner awareness (not a blocker): the `Contact` field is the instance's `CommunityOptions.SupportEmail` — the owner should confirm the **prod** `SupportEmail` is a **real, monitored** address (the same open item F12 carries, pin §7(a)); if unconfigured on a live instance the field falls back to the stable `mailto:security@localhost` floor (non-blank, RFC 9116-valid, but not a real monitored mailbox — OPS-05 would be met in form, not in substance).
- **Invariants held:** no `Kumunita.Core/**` change (the `CommunityOptions` Core type is **read** by the Web controller — ADR 0006 Web→Core direction, the F12 `SupportEmailAlertSink` precedent); no new `<PackageReference>`; no new bounded context / DocTypes surface; no migration; no `.slnx` change; anonymous by default (the app has **no** global authorization filter — `Program.cs:40` is `AddControllersWithViews()` with no `RequireAuthenticatedUser`, the `StaticPagesController` routes are likewise unauthenticated readers — so the route needs no `[AllowAnonymous]`); the F7 CSP middleware, the F9/F10/F12 gated blocks, and every existing controller are **untouched**.

## F13.U2 — `SecurityTxt_ReturnsValidFields` test (LAST UNIT OF F13)
- **Landed (1 file):** `tests/Kumunita.Web.Tests/F13U2_SecurityTxtTests.cs` (new, ~210 LOC) — the **one** pinned test `SecurityTxt_ReturnsValidFields` (an `[Fact]`, **synchronous** — the action is `ContentResult`, not `Task<...>`; no `TestContext.Current.CancellationToken` needed since the action takes none). A private `Field(body, name)` helper extracts an RFC 9116 `Field: value` line and fails the test if the field is absent (all five fields are mandatory). **No `src/**` change** (the F13.U1 `WellKnownController` + `SECURITY-VDP.md` are **untouched**); no `<PackageReference>`; no migration; no `.slnx` change.
- **Pins relied on:** F13.U1 handoff note "Pinned test name" section (the 200/text/plain + five-field asserts + the future-Expires leg + the direct-action-call idiom); the `WellKnownController` source (the `IOptions<CommunityOptions>` ctor dep + the `Request.Scheme`/`Request.Host` `Policy` build + the `security@localhost` floor — confirmed matching the pin exactly).
- **The six assertion legs (all green):**
  1. `Assert.IsType<ContentResult>`; `StatusCode` **null-or-200** (the `<c>Content(body, "text/plain")</c>` helper leaves `StatusCode` at the ASP.NET Core "use the framework default" sentinel `null`, which the `ContentResultExecutor` resolves to **200** — the semantic "200 OK" contract, not a literal-200 string assert); `ContentType` contains `text/plain`.
  2. `Contact:` starts `mailto:` **and** equals `mailto:support@examplium.com` (the stubbed `CommunityOptions.SupportEmail`).
  3. **Load-bearing leg** — `Expires:` value **parses** to a date **> 12 months from `DateTime.UtcNow`** (a parse-then-compare, **not** a string-equality assert, so the pinned date can roll without breaking the test).
  4. `Policy:` starts `http://localhost` (the stubbed origin) **and** ends `/.well-known/security.txt`.
  5. `Preferred-Languages:` equals `en`.
  6. `Hints:` is non-empty.
- **Harness idiom used (the F3.U5 / `KioskControllerTests` precedent, per the F13.U1 pin's "direct action call, not a TestServer"):** `new WellKnownController(Options.Create(new CommunityOptions { SupportEmail = "support@examplium.com" }))` + a `DefaultHttpContext` with `Request.Scheme = "http"` + `Host = new HostString("localhost")` set on the `ControllerContext` (the exact `KioskControllerTests` L365-383 stub shape). No `ServiceCollection`, no `IUrlHelper` stub needed (the controller has no `UrlHelper` dependency — its only DI dep is `IOptions<CommunityOptions>`).
- **One in-unit build fix (not a pin divergence, recorded here for transparency):** my **first** leg-1 assert was `Assert.Equal(200, content.StatusCode)` — that **failed** (`Expected: 200, Actual: null`). The `Content(body, "text/plain")` helper produces a `ContentResult` whose `StatusCode` is the framework-default sentinel (`null`), **not** a literal `200`. The route is genuinely a 200 (the `ContentResultExecutor` resolves `null` → 200). I corrected the leg-1 assert to `content.StatusCode is null || content.StatusCode == 200` (the semantic "200 OK" contract). This is the **correct** interpretation of the pin's "returns 200" — the pin fixes the **contract** (a 200 response), not the literal `StatusCode` property value. **No `src/**` change was required** — the `WellKnownController` is correct as-is; only the test's leg-1 assert needed the framework-semantics nuance.
- **Test result:**
  - `dotnet build Kumunita.slnx -c Debug` — **Build succeeded, 0 Errors** (47 warnings, all **pre-existing** — the `F5U4_KillAllTests.cs` CS8766 / `F9U4_SignupTests.cs` xUnit1051 / `Kumunita.Core` CS8603/CS8714 / view warnings; **none** from my file).

## F14.U1 — Part-0 design pin for the CI test gate (FIRST UNIT OF F14)
- **Landed (exactly ONE file, docs-only; **no `src/**` change, no `.github/` change, no `<PackageReference>`, no migration, no `.slnx` change, no build run**):** `docs/design/prod-hardening-f14-ci.md` (**NEW**, all 8 sections) — the **Part-0 design pin** for the DEP-03 CI test gate. Pins: §1 current state (`.github/` holds **only** `copilot-instructions.md` — **no** `workflows/` dir, **no** CI today); §2 the **three verbatim `dotnet` step commands** (the load-bearing leg, § below); §3 the branch (`release`) + trigger (push + PR to `release`); §4 the gate contract (a non-zero exit from the build OR **either** dll blocks the deploy; the check is a **required** status check, not a warning); §5 the Core Testcontainers note (Docker needed; **no** `docker container prune` in CI — it's a local step); §6 the **U3 owner open item** (wire Coolify to *require* the green check — **recorded, not assumed**); §7 the assert shape for U2 (no xUnit test — U2 asserts the file exists + the three commands are present in order + no `dotnet test`); §8 the invariants + exit contract.
- **The three pinned `dotnet` step commands (U2 transcribes these VERBATIM, in this order):**
  ```bash
  dotnet build Kumunita.slnx -c Debug
  dotnet exec tests/Kumunita.Web.Tests/bin/Debug/net10.0/Kumunita.Web.Tests.dll
  dotnet exec tests/Kumunita.Core.Tests/bin/Debug/net10.0/Kumunita.Core.Tests.dll
  ```
  (The **in-process `dotnet exec`** path, **NOT** `dotnet test` — the anti-regression pin. The `net10.0`/`Debug` path segment is load-bearing. CI plumbing before the three steps: `checkout` + install the .NET 10 SDK matching `global.json` (`sdk.version = 10.0.400`, `rollForward = latestFeature`), e.g. `dotnet/setup-dotnet@v4` — that is CI plumbing, not a pinned gate command, not a `<PackageReference>`.)
- **Gate contract (the load-bearing leg):** the CI job is green **iff** `build == 0` AND `Web.Tests == 0` AND `Core.Tests == 0`; **any** non-zero (build, Web.Tests, or Core.Tests) → red → the `release` check is not green → the deploy that *requires* that green check does not run. A green Web.Tests does **not** offset a red Core.Tests. The check is a **required** status check on `release` (U3 makes it required; U2 makes it exist + accurate).
- **Core Testcontainers note (pin §5):** `Kumunita.Core.Tests` needs a working Docker daemon (starts `postgres:18` via Testcontainers). GitHub-hosted runners have Docker pre-installed (no action). **Do NOT add `docker container prune -f` to the CI workflow** — it is a **local** cleanup concern (a killed local runner); a CI runner is **ephemeral** (fresh container per run, recycled after) so there is no persistent leak and the prune is noise/no-op in CI. The prune stays a local instruction (`AGENTS.md`), not a CI step.
- **U3 owner open item (the `code+ops` half — **recorded, NOT assumed done**):** wire the **Coolify deploy** to **require** the green CI check on `release` (deploy precondition / `release` branch protection = required status check); confirm **Docker is available** on the CI runner; confirm the branch protection enforces the required check (blocks a force-push that skips it). **Until U3 runs, the check *exists* (push shows green/red) but does *not* block a deploy** — the gate is only as strong as U3's "require" wiring.
- **Pinned assert shape for U2 (there is NO xUnit test in F14 — noted in the pin §7):** U2 asserts the **one** new file `.github/workflows/ci.yml` **exists**, contains the **three pinned `dotnet` commands in order** (§2), the **`release` push+PR trigger** (§3), and **no `dotnet test`** (the anti-regression pin) + **no `docker container prune`** (the §5 pin). U2's "build green + test green" exit reduces to **"the `ci.yml` file is written and internally consistent with pin §2–§5"** — there is no test to run (U2 does not need to run the workflow; a real `release` push / U3's wiring does that).
- **Drift pause:** **none** — this is a **design-pin unit** (docs-only, no code to read against the codebase). Grounded in: `AGENTS.md` § "Running the tests (test-runner quirk)" (the verbatim build → `dotnet exec` Web.Tests → `dotnet exec` Core.Tests path + the "leaves Docker containers behind if the process is killed — clean up with `docker container prune`" local note), `global.json` (`sdk.version = 10.0.400`, `rollForward = latestFeature`, `test.runner = Microsoft.Testing.Platform`), and the `plan-prod-hardening.md` F14 section (U1/U2/U3 split, `code+ops`, U3 owner open item). `.github/` was **verified** to hold only `copilot-instructions.md` (no existing workflow to diverge from).
- **Test result:** **N/A — design-pin unit (no code, no build run).** The exit contract is the doc's internal consistency (met: §2 names the exact three `dotnet` commands verbatim; §3–§7 are internally consistent; §8 confirms the ≤1-file / no-`src/**` / no-close-flip invariants).
- **F14 is now at U2 (F14.U1 COMPLETE — the Part-0 design pin).** The prod-hardening lane stays **open** — **F14.U2, F14.U3, F15–F22 remain**. **No 6-member close-flip** (that happens at **F22**, the lane's last unit). Next unit = **F14.U2** (`code`, the **one** file `.github/workflows/ci.yml`) — a fresh clean-slate agent. **F14.U2 MUST read the pin's §2 (the three verbatim commands + the in-process-not-`dotnet test` pin + the `net10.0`/`Debug` path) + §3 (branch + trigger) + §4 (gate contract) + §5 (no `docker container prune` in CI) + §7 (the assert shape)** — **and transcribe the §2 commands verbatim** (that is the whole value of this pin).

Workflow not run (a real `release` push / U3's wiring exercises it).

## F15.U1 — Part-0 design pin (FIRST UNIT OF F15)
- **Landed (exactly ONE new file; **no `src/**` change, no `<PackageReference>`, no migration, no `.slnx` change, no build run, no test run**):** `docs/design/prod-hardening-f15-lifecycle.md` (new, §1–§9). Design-only.
- **Retention rule chosen (pin §2): 365 days, a platform constant (NOT a config knob).** Grounded in the repo's two live precedents: `UsagePurgeService.RetentionDays = 365` (ADR 0114 D5 "a platform constant", `src/Kumunita.Core/Usage/UsagePurgeService.cs`) and `StorageMetricsCaptureService.RetentionDays = 365` (ADR 0156 M33·7 "a per-instance knob is a named deferral", `src/Kumunita.Core/Usage/StorageMetricsCaptureService.cs`). **Where it lives (U2):** a NEW `public const int RetentionDays = 365;` on the new `MediaLifecycleService` (in `Kumunita.Core`), NOT reused from `UsagePurgeService`. Cutoff = `now.AddDays(-RetentionDays)`; expired iff `MediaObject.Created < cutoff`; `now` is a `DateTimeOffset` injection param (the `UsagePurgeService.PurgeAsync` shape).
- **Tick/lock shape pinned (pin §3) — mirrors the existing tick+service+handler+seed house shape, verbatim:** (1) `MediaLifecycleTick()` : `Wolverine.TimeoutMessage(TimeSpan.FromDays(1))` in `Kumunita.Core/Media/` (the `UsagePurgeTick`/`StorageMetricsCaptureTick` shape, 1-day delay baked in, no per-callsite `DelayedFor`); (2) a **Wolverine-free static** `MediaLifecycleService.SweepAsync(IDocumentStore, IMediaFileStore, DateTimeOffset now, CancellationToken)` in `Kumunita.Core/Media/` (ADR 0006-D, the `UsagePurgeService`/`StorageMetricsCaptureService` shape); (3) a thin `MediaLifecycleHandler` in `Kumunita.Web/SideEffects/` returning `Task<IEnumerable<object>>` and re-publishing a fresh tick (the `UsagePurgeHandler` shape); (4) **one line** `await bus.PublishAsync(new MediaLifecycleTick());` in `Program.cs`'s existing "Kick off the recurring §6.4 jobs on boot" block (the `UsagePurgeTick`/`StorageMetricsCaptureTick` seed precedent). **Idempotency = natural no-op + `DeleteIfExistsAsync` idempotency + single-commit batched shape + Postgres-backed `TimeoutMessage` durability** (NOT a Postgres advisory lock — see drift below).
- **Exact F1 byte-delete seam U2 re-uses (pin §4):** `IMediaFileStore.DeleteIfExistsAsync(string contentId, CancellationToken ct = default)` (F1.U2, confirmed present in `src/Kumunita.Core/Media/IMediaFileStore.cs`) — idempotent, missing file = no-op returns `false`, never throws. **Byte key = the `MediaObject` row's `Id`** (lowercase-hex SHA-256 content id, confirmed in `MediaObject.cs`); **owner field `CreatedById`** (named for completeness — F15's predicate is `Created < cutoff` only, NOT owner-scoped like F1). **Do NOT use `DeleteFileAsync`** (fail-closed, throws on missing). Algorithm (pin §5): collect expired ids → one session `Delete<MediaObject>` + ONE `SaveChangesAsync` → **post-commit** `DeleteIfExistsAsync` per id (try/catch, log+swallow, F1.U4 C-MED·7 orphan-safe) → return count.
- **Pinned test name (U3 implements, pin §6):** `Lifecycle_DeletesExpiredBytes` — plant an expired `MediaObject` row + byte (real `LocalVolumeFileStore` on a unique temp dir; F1.U5 GOTCHA-1: boot store must call `MediaDocTypes.Configure(opts)`, `MediaObject` is on the **`MediaDocTypes`** surface) + a not-expired control row/byte; run `SweepAsync` once; assert (1) expired row gone, (2) `media.ExistsAsync(expiredId) == false`, (3) a **second** `SweepAsync` returns 0 and the not-expired row+byte remain (no double-run + keeps-others-intact).
- **Drift pause (recorded, NOT guessed — owner to confirm at U1-review):** the plan's F15 spec names "a Postgres advisory lock" as the idempotency precedent. **There is NO advisory lock anywhere in `src/`** (full-`src` grep `pg_advisory`/`AdvisoryLock`/`lock_id` → 0 hits) and **none of the five existing jobs** (`AuditPurge`/`UsagePurge`/`EventReminder`/`StorageMetricsCapture`/`NotificationFlush`) takes one. The pin (§3.5) uses the **real** mechanism — what "mirror the existing tick+service pairs" directs. If the owner specifically wants an explicit advisory lock (to harden against simultaneous double-schedule from two live replicas), that is a **shape change + a decision + a new frozen seam + a lock-table migration**, to be made at U1-review — **not** silently added. This is a pin-vs-reality reconciliation, not a codebase-divergence stop.
- **Test result:** **N/A — no code in F15.U1 (design-only).** Exit = the pin's internal consistency: retention rule pinned (365-day constant, §2); tick/lock/idempotent shape pinned against the repo precedent (§3); F1's `DeleteIfExistsAsync` seam named exactly (§4); `Lifecycle_DeletesExpiredBytes` + its 3 assertions pinned (§6); the one drift (advisory-lock precedent) recorded, not guessed (§8.1).
- **Open items (owner, **recorded, NOT assumed done**):** confirm at U1-review that no explicit Postgres advisory lock is wanted (drift, pin §8.1) — default pinned = natural-no-op + `DeleteIfExistsAsync` idempotency. If a lock IS wanted, it changes U2's shape.
- **F15 is now at U2 (F15.U1 COMPLETE — the one design pin).** The prod-hardening lane stays **open** — **F15.U2–U3 + F16–F22 remain**. **No 6-member close-flip** (that happens at **F22**, the lane's last unit). The next unit = **F15.U2** (the `MediaLifecycleTick` job: `MediaLifecycleTick.cs` + `MediaLifecycleService.cs` in `Kumunita.Core/Media/`, the Web `MediaLifecycleHandler.cs`, + the one `Program.cs` seed line — the house shape; ≤2 `Kumunita.Core` files) — a fresh clean-slate agent.

## F15.U2 — the `MediaLifecycleTick` job (SECOND UNIT OF F15)
- **Landed (4 touchpoints — 2 `Kumunita.Core` files + 2 Web glue, per pin §3.4/§8.3; **no `<PackageReference>`, no migration, no `.slnx` change**):**
  - `src/Kumunita.Core/Media/MediaLifecycleTick.cs` — NEW, `public sealed record MediaLifecycleTick() : Wolverine.TimeoutMessage(TimeSpan.FromDays(1));` (the `UsagePurgeTick` shape verbatim — 1-day delay baked in, no per-callsite `DelayedFor`).
  - `src/Kumunita.Core/Media/MediaLifecycleService.cs` — NEW, **static** Wolverine-free (HTTP-free, ADR 0006-D) service: `public const int RetentionDays = 365;` (a **distinct** constant, NOT reused from `UsagePurgeService`, per pin §2) + `public static async Task<int> SweepAsync(IDocumentStore, IMediaFileStore, DateTimeOffset now, CancellationToken)`.
  - `src/Kumunita.Web/SideEffects/MediaLifecycleHandler.cs` — NEW, thin adapter mirroring `UsagePurgeHandler`: `Task<IEnumerable<object>> Handle(MediaLifecycleTick, IDocumentStore, IMediaFileStore)` → `await MediaLifecycleService.SweepAsync(store, media, DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);` then `return new[] { new MediaLifecycleTick() };` (returns the array, not `yield return` — the async-eligible cascade shape).
  - `src/Kumunita.Web/Program.cs` — **one line** added to the existing "Kick off the recurring §6.4 jobs … on boot" block, beside the `UsagePurgeTick`/`StorageMetricsCaptureTick` seeds: `await bus.PublishAsync(new MediaLifecycleTick());` (with the F15/UPL-04 explanatory comment; `using Kumunita.Core.Media;` was already present).
- **Tick/lock/idempotent shape as implemented (pin §3/§3.5):** self-rescheduling `Wolverine.TimeoutMessage(1 day)` + static Wolverine-free service + thin Web handler + boot seed — the exact five-job house shape. **Idempotency = natural no-op (a double-run selects the already-gone set → zero ids) + `DeleteIfExistsAsync` no-op-on-missing + single-commit batched shape + Postgres-backed `TimeoutMessage` durability.** **No Postgres advisory lock** (pin §8.1 drift — confirmed 0 hits in `src/`, none of the five precedent jobs use one; adding one would be a new frozen seam beyond the pin, which the unit-series rules forbid).
- **The `DeleteIfExistsAsync` seam re-used (pin §4, F1.U2):** called **as-is, not reshaped** — `media.DeleteIfExistsAsync(id, ct)` per expired row, byte key = the `MediaObject` row's `Id` (lowercase-hex SHA-256 content id, confirmed in `MediaObject.cs`). **`DeleteFileAsync` NOT used** (fail-closed; would throw on an already-deleted byte). **Predicate is `MediaObject.Created < cutoff` only** (a time-based sweep of *all* expired rows — **not** owner-scoped like F1; `CreatedById` is NOT a predicate here).
- **Delete algorithm as implemented (pin §5):** (1) `cutoff = now.AddDays(-RetentionDays)`; (2) **one** `IDocumentStore` session — `session.Query<MediaObject>().Where(o => o.Created < cutoff).Select(o => o.Id).ToListAsync(ct)` → `.Distinct().ToList()` → `session.Delete<MediaObject>(id)` each → **one** `SaveChangesAsync` (catalog-row deletes committed FIRST — C3 single-write-lane, ADR 0006); (3) **post-commit** byte deletes — `foreach (id) { try { await media.DeleteIfExistsAsync(id, ct); } catch { /* log + continue */ } }` (a byte-delete failure is swallowed, never rethrown — the C-MED·7 orphan-safe invariant, mirroring F1.U4); (4) `return expired.Count` (the catalog-row delete count, the `UsagePurgeService` "return the purge count" shape).
- **Invariants held (pin §7):** Core stays HTTP-free (no Web types / `IHttpContextAccessor` in `MediaLifecycleService`); no new bounded context / no new doc type / no `MediaDocTypes` surface change; no new frozen seam (only `DeleteIfExistsAsync` re-used); single-write-lane one-commit (one session, one `SaveChangesAsync`); orphan-safe (post-commit byte deletes, try/catch swallow).
- **Test result (per the repo test quirk in `AGENTS.md` — `dotnet exec` of the test dlls, NOT `dotnet test`):**
  - `dotnet build Kumunita.slnx -c Debug` — **Build succeeded, 0 Warning(s), 0 Error(s).** (the sync terminal channel wedged once — the known Copilot bug — re-run via `mode=async` + `tee` per the AGENTS.md workaround.)
  - `dotnet exec tests/Kumunita.Web.Tests/.../Kumunita.Web.Tests.dll` — **Total: 1074, Errors: 0, Failed: 0, Skipped: 1, exit 0.**
  - `dotnet exec tests/Kumunita.Core.Tests/.../Kumunita.Core.Tests.dll` — **Total: 1559, Errors: 0, Failed: 0, Skipped: 2, exit 0** (Testcontainers; `docker container prune -f` afterward reported "Total reclaimed space: 0B" — the run had already cleaned up its containers).
- **Drift pause:** none — the `DeleteIfExistsAsync` seam (pin §4), the `UsagePurgeTick`/`UsagePurgeService`/`UsagePurgeHandler` house shape, the `MediaObject` `Id`/`Created`/`CreatedById` fields, and the `Program.cs` seed block all matched the pin exactly. The only recorded drift is the pin's own §8.1 (advisory-lock precedent does not exist in the repo) — which the pin already reconciled and U2 followed (no lock added).
- **Open items:** none (a pure `code` unit — no owner-ops step). Next unit = **F15.U3** (the `Lifecycle_DeletesExpiredBytes` test, pin §6 — plant an expired + a not-expired `MediaObject` row/byte against a real `LocalVolumeFileStore` on a unique temp dir with `MediaDocTypes.Configure(opts)` in the boot store; run `SweepAsync`; assert the three pinned assertions incl. the second-run-returns-0 no-double-run check) — a fresh clean-slate agent.
- **F15 is now at U3 (F15.U2 COMPLETE — the house-shape job landed, build green, both test suites green).** The prod-hardening lane stays **open** — **F15.U3 + F16–F22 remain**. **No 6-member close-flip** (that happens at **F22**, the lane's last unit).

## F15.U3 — the `Lifecycle_DeletesExpiredBytes` test (THIRD UNIT OF F15)
- **Landed (one test file, **no new package, no migration, no `.slnx` change, no `src/**` change**):**
  - `tests/Kumunita.Core.Tests/Media/MediaLifecycleTests.cs` — NEW, a single pinned test `Lifecycle_DeletesExpiredBytes` (the **unrenamed** pin §6 name — the next audit run diffs against it). Namespace `Kumunita.Core.Tests.Media` (matching the sibling `Media/` test files).
- **The three pinned assertions as implemented (pin §6, verbatim):**
  1. **expired catalog row is gone** — `Assert.Null(await LoadAsync(store, expiredId))` after the sweep (a fresh `QuerySession`, `LoadAsync<MediaObject>` returns null → the `session.Delete<MediaObject>(id)` + `SaveChangesAsync` committed).
  2. **expired byte is gone** — `Assert.False(await media.ExistsAsync(expiredId))` (the byte genuinely deleted from the temp dir by `DeleteIfExistsAsync`, asserted against a **real** `LocalVolumeFileStore`, not a null-skip).
  3. **no double-run / keeps-others-intact** — a **second** `SweepAsync(store, media, now, ct)` returns **`0`** (`Assert.Equal(0, second)`), and the **not-expired** control row (`Assert.NotNull(await LoadAsync(store, liveId))`) + byte (`Assert.True(await media.ExistsAsync(liveId))`) are **still present**.
  - **Plant (pin §6 shape):** two fresh 64-char lowercase-hex content ids (`SHA256.HashData` of a GUID, `ToLowerInvariant` — the byte-store key, mirroring F1.U5's `NewContentId`); bytes planted first via `media.PutAsync(id, …, ct)` (real files on the volume); then a `MediaObject` row each — the expired one `Created = now.AddDays(-(MediaLifecycleService.RetentionDays + 1))` (= 366 days, **past** the 365-day cutoff so it is expired, pin §2) and the control one `Created = now` (not expired). Precondition asserts (`ExistsAsync` true on both) guard against a vacuous byte test.
- **Harness setup (pin §8.2 GOTCHA-1 + GOTCHA-2, both held):** the boot store calls **`MediaDocTypes.Configure(opts)`** (plus `opts.DatabaseSchemaName = "mt"`) — `MediaObject` lives on the **`MediaDocTypes`** surface, **not** `CoreDocTypes`/`PostDocTypes`; without it the query hits an unmanaged-table error. A **real** `LocalVolumeFileStore` is constructed against a **unique temp dir** (`Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "kumunita-lifecycle-" + Guid …)`) so the post-commit byte-delete genuinely runs and is assertable — **not** null-skipped. Harness mirrors the F1.U5 `PostgresFixture` + `NewDatabaseAsync` (fresh scratch DB) shape.
- **Test result (per the repo test quirk in `AGENTS.md` — `dotnet exec` of the test dlls, NOT `dotnet test`; the sync terminal channel wedged repeatedly — the known Copilot bug — so runs were driven via `mode=async` + log-file + read):**
  - `dotnet build Kumunita.slnx -c Debug` — **Build succeeded** (0 errors; the only errors seen on the first build were my own `LoadAsync<MediaObject>` type-arg call sites, fixed to the non-generic helper — rebuild green).
  - `dotnet exec tests/Kumunita.Core.Tests/.../Kumunita.Core.Tests.dll` — **Total: 1560, Errors: 0, Failed: 0, Skipped: 2, exit 0.** (the **+1** over F15.U2's 1559 is exactly this new test; a failure would show `Failed: N>0`.)
  - `dotnet exec tests/Kumunita.Web.Tests/.../Kumunita.Web.Tests.dll` — **Total: 1074, Errors: 0, Failed: 0, Skipped: 1, exit 0.**
  - `docker container prune -f` afterward → "Total reclaimed space: 0B" (the runs already disposed their Testcontainers on shutdown).

The green aggregate (`Failed: 0`) with the +1 test count is the confirmation that `Lifecycle_DeletesExpiredBytes` ran and passed. The U2 implementation was **not** touched (as instructed — any concern there would be an F15.U2 issue).

## F16.U1 — Part-0 design pin (FIRST UNIT OF F16)
- **Landed (exactly ONE new file; **no `src/**` change, no `<PackageReference>`, no migration, no `.slnx` change, no build run, no test run**):** `docs/design/prod-hardening-f16-scan.md` (**NEW**, §1–§9). **Design-only** (the F15.U1 / F14.U1 / F12.U1 design-pin unit shape — one design doc, no code).
- **The four pins (per the plan's F16 section), each grounded in a real repo precedent (not invented):**
  - **§2 — the scanner + seam shape:** **ClamAV via the store (a local daemon), NOT a third-party HTTP provider.** Why (recorded): self-hosted/T1 (ADR 0002) → a local `clamd` is the natural fit with **no external dependency** (no API key, no PII egress — the privacy-first model in `SECURITY.md`); **ADR 0006-D — Core stays HTTP-free** (a provider would need `HttpClient` in `Kumunita.Core`, where the seam lives — ClamAV-via-daemon is a local process/socket, the `ISmtpSender` "Core I/O-seam / Web transport" split); the audit already assumes ClamAV (the open item is "run ClamAV on the prod volume"). **The seam:** a new `IVirusScanner` in `Kumunita.Core/Media/`, `Task<VirusScanStatus> ScanAsync(Stream content, CancellationToken ct)` + a `VirusScanStatus` enum (`Clean`/`Infected`). **`Stream` (not `contentId`+store) so it is store-agnostic and trivially stubbable** (U3's stub returns `Infected`, no real byte). The scanner re-uses the serve lane's **existing** `IMediaStore.OpenReadAsync` read seam (`IMediaStore.cs:14`) — **no new byte-read seam, no `IMediaFileStore` change**. **§2.1 — the scanner-unavailable default: fail-OPEN (recorded, owner may flip)** — a missing/errored daemon returns `Clean` (a missing optional daemon must not DoS the serve lane; consistent with C-MED·7 "degrade to a 404, not a crash" + the F10/F12 "off-by-default floor" idiom; the gate becomes *real* only once the owner deploys ClamAV). Fail-closed (the alternative) is **NOT** pinned — it would DoS all media serving on any box without ClamAV (incl. dev/scratch). **Recorded, NOT guessed** — owner may flip at U1-review (a one-line change in the default impl, not the seam shape or the test).
  - **§3 — the quarantine state:** a new `bool Quarantined` on the **existing** `MediaObject` POCO (default `false`; `MediaObject.cs:13`). Not a new doc / not a new surface — a **property on the doc the serve lane already loads** (must survive a `pg_dump` restore — C-MED·4). **Set at SERVE time (§4), NOT at upload time** (the invariant names the serve lane as the enforcement point; upload-time scanning is the §1 out-of-F16 scope fence). Idempotent: a later serve sees `Quarantined == true` **before** re-scanning and short-circuits to 404. **The `MediaDocTypes` GOTCHA (F1.U5 GOTCHA-1, carried verbatim):** `MediaObject` is on the **`MediaDocTypes`** surface (not `CoreDocTypes`/`PostDocTypes`) — U3's harness **must** call `MediaDocTypes.Configure(opts)` in the boot store setup (`MediaLifecycleTests.cs:116` precedent) — else an unmanaged-table error. The `MediaDocTypes.Configure` registration `opts.Schema.For<MediaObject>()` (`MediaDocTypes.cs:23`) is **unchanged** — a new POCO property, Marten applies the column idempotently at boot (ADR 0004 §B).
  - **§4 — the enforcement point (the serve lane), grounded in the real controllers:** the **three** serve controllers + the exact insertion point (immediately **after** the step-2 store-miss 404, **before** the reverse-lookup / blob serve): `AttachmentController.Serve` (`GET /attachment/{id}`, after `if (stored is null) return NotFound();`), `ContentImageController.Serve` (`GET /content-image/{id}`, after the step-2 miss ⇒ 404), `DocumentController.Serve` (`GET /documents/{id}/download`, after the blob-miss `if (stored is null) return NotFound();`). **The 404-not-served contract: `NotFound()` (404), NOT 403 — zero audit rows** (the repo's no-leak posture, the **exact** posture the serve lane already uses for a store miss (404, zero rows) and a `Deny` decision (404, not 403 — the `PostReadDecision` precedent); a quarantine refusal is a store-level refusal, so **no** `AccessAudit` row — same reasoning as the store-miss / announcement scope-deny paths, `AttachmentController.cs` step-2/step-4 docs). Placing the gate **before** the `Read` decision means the malware byte is refused for **everyone** (an anonymous visitor to a public post's attachment included) without burning an audit row.
  - **§6 — the pinned test `InfectedUpload_NotServed`:** the **single** F16-pinned test (plan F16.U3: "a stub scanner returns infected, the serve lane 404s"). Web.Tests (the F12.U3 / F9.U4 / F10.U3 "Web class → Web.Tests" precedent); the pinned lane = **`AttachmentController.Serve`** (the literal "upload served as a download" path UPL-02 names — the other two controllers get the **same one-line gate** in U2; U3 may assert any, but **this name + this lane is pinned**). Harness: a **real** `IMediaStore` (a `LocalVolumeMediaStore` over a temp-dir `LocalVolumeFileStore`, the F1.U5 / F15.U3 real-byte-store pattern — not a null-skip) + a **stub** `IVirusScanner` returning `Infected` + a `MediaObject` with `Quarantined = false`; **the boot store MUST call `MediaDocTypes.Configure(opts)`** (the §3 GOTCHA). **The pinned legs:** (1) the serve action returns **404 / `NotFound`** (not a 403, not a `FileResult` — the not-served contract); (2) the `MediaObject` row's `Quarantined` is **`true`** after the serve (a fresh read); (3) a **second** serve 404s **without re-invoking the scanner** (the §3 short-circuit — a recording stub asserts the `ScanAsync` call count is still 1). (4) optional control: a `Clean`-verdict byte is served + `Quarantined` stays `false`. **Do NOT rename it** (the next audit run diffs against it).
- **The owner-ops open item (recorded, NOT assumed done — the `code+ops` half of F16):** **run ClamAV on the prod volume** (install `clamd`, point the default scanner impl at it, confirm it scans the upload volume). **Until this runs, the serve gate is fail-open (a no-op on a missing daemon)** — the browser-side `nosniff` + `attachment` mitigation (which stays) is the active control. Mirrors the F10 `SENTRY_DSN` / F12 `SupportEmail` "recorded, not assumed" idiom (the owner runs it on the prod box; **not** silently assumed done).
- **Precedent files cited (all confirmed matching the pin — no drift):** the three serve controllers `src/Kumunita.Web/Controllers/AttachmentController.cs` (the `Serve` 5-step ordering, step-2 store-miss 404, step-5 `File(stream, stored.ContentType)` + `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff` + `Content-Disposition: attachment|inline`), `ContentImageController.cs` (the step-2 miss ⇒ 404, step-5 `OpenReadAsync` → `File` + `nosniff`), `DocumentController.cs` (the `/download` step-2 + blob-miss 404 + the `OpenReadAsync` serve); the Media seam `src/Kumunita.Core/Media/MediaObject.cs` (the POCO: `Id`/`Filename`/`ContentType`/`SizeBytes`/`Created`/`CreatedById`), `IMediaStore.cs` (`OpenReadAsync` at `:14`, `PutAsync`/`GetAsync`/`RemoveAsync`), `IMediaFileStore.cs` (`OpenReadAsync`/`DeleteIfExistsAsync` — **not** re-used by F16), `LocalVolumeFileStore.cs` (`OpenReadAsync` at `:44`); the `MediaDocTypes` surface `src/Kumunita.Core/MediaDocTypes.cs` (`Configure(opts)` → `opts.Schema.For<MediaObject>()`); the F15 design pin `docs/design/prod-hardening-f15-lifecycle.md` (the **house-shape template** — §1 scope fence / §2 pinned rule / §3 shape / §4 seam / §5 algorithm / §6 pinned test / §7 invariants / §8 drift / §9 exit — mirrored here).
- **Drift (recorded, NOT guessed):** **no scanner / quarantine / ClamAV / `Infected` exists anywhere in `src/`** (full-`src` grep `ClamAV|Virus|IVirusScanner|Quarantine|IsInfected|Infected|AntiMalware` → **0 hits**) — a **genuinely new** seam; no existing expectation to reconcile, **no production defect found** (no `## F16.U1 — Drift pause` raised). The **three** recorded reconciliations (pin §8): (1) the **fail-open/fail-closed default** (§2.1 — the scanner is not yet deployed; the pin chooses fail-open, owner may flip at U1-review); (2) the **`MediaDocTypes` surface** (U3's harness GOTCHA); (3) the **`IMediaStore.OpenReadAsync` read-seam choice** (F16 re-uses the seam it is *in*; F15 re-used `IMediaFileStore.DeleteIfExistsAsync` — each fix re-uses its own lane's seam, no cross-fix); (4) **upload-time scanning is OUT of F16** (the invariant names the serve lane as the enforcement point; upload-time is a separate decision + a write-path shape change, made at U1-review, not silently added).
- **Test result:** **N/A — this is a design-pin unit; the exit contract is NO build, NO test run** (the F15.U1 / F14.U1 / F12.U1 design-pin unit exit-contract idiom). The pin's §7 build/test command is recorded for F16.U2 / F16.U3 (per `AGENTS.md` test quirk — `dotnet exec` of the test dlls, NOT `dotnet test`): `dotnet build Kumunita.slnx -c Debug`, then `dotnet exec tests/Kumunita.Web.Tests/bin/Debug/net10.0/Kumunita.Web.Tests.dll` (the F16.U3 test assembly) and `dotnet exec tests/Kumunita.Core.Tests/bin/Debug/net10.0/Kumunita.Core.Tests.dll` (the no-regression confirm; `docker container prune -f` after the Core run). **F16.U1 does NOT run either.**
- **Open items (owner, **recorded, NOT assumed done**):** **run ClamAV on the prod volume** (the `code+ops` half — see above). **The one owner awareness (not a blocker):** the §2.1 fail-open default is a **recorded decision** — if the owner wants fail-closed, that is a one-line change in U2's default-impl + a decision at U1-review (the seam shape / quarantine flag / serve-lane 404 contract / the pinned test are **all unchanged** either way).
- **Invariants held (pin §7):** Core stays HTTP-free (ADR 0006-D — the seam is a pure "bytes in → verdict out" contract, no `HttpClient`; the default impl drives a local daemon); **the serve lane is the enforcement point** (NOT the upload lane — the upload / `PutAsync` write path, the M24 quota gate, the C-MED·7 orphan-safe ordering are **untouched**); **no new bounded context / no new doc type** (one bool on the existing `MediaObject`, the `MediaDocTypes` registration unchanged; one new seam `IVirusScanner`; three one-line gates); C3 single-write-lane / one-commit (the flag-write is one `SaveChangesAsync`); the no-leak 404 posture (404, not 403, zero audit rows); the existing serve mitigation STAYS (`attachment` + `nosniff` untouched); the fail-open default is recorded, not guessed; **no new `<PackageReference>` in U1** (the seam is a plain interface + enum).
- **F16 is now at U2 (F16.U1 COMPLETE — the one design pin).** The prod-hardening lane stays **open** — **F16.U2–U3 + F17–F22 remain**. **No 6-member close-flip** (that happens at **F22**, the lane's last unit). Next unit = **F16.U2** (the `IVirusScanner` seam + the `MediaObject.Quarantined` bool + the three serve-lane gates — ≤3 files: the Core seam file, the `MediaObject` bool, and the serve-controller gate(s) — the Web glue the "serve lane is the enforcement point" invariant requires) — a fresh clean-slate agent. **F16.U2 MUST read the pin's §2 (the seam + the fail-open/fail-closed default + the owner-ops item) + §3 (the quarantine flag + the `MediaDocTypes` GOTCHA) + §4/§5 (the enforcement point + the algorithm + the exact insertion points in the three controllers) + §6 (the test name to keep consistent) + §7 (the invariants) + §8 (the drift notes).**

## F16.U2 — the UPL-02 upload-scan seam + quarantine flag + serve-lane gates (SECOND UNIT OF F16)
- **Landed (the pin's four decisions, §2/§3/§4/§5 implemented; ≤3 logical files + the Web glue the invariant requires + the DI registration point the seam needs):**
  1. **`src/Kumunita.Core/Media/IVirusScanner.cs` (NEW)** — `VirusScanStatus` enum (`Clean`/`Infected`) + `interface IVirusScanner { Task<VirusScanStatus> ScanAsync(Stream content, CancellationToken ct = default); }` (HTTP-free, ADR 0006-D — "bytes in → verdict out", no `HttpClient`) **+ the default fail-open impl `ClamAvScanner`** (a **local** `clamd` INSTREAM driver over a loopback `TcpClient` — **no `<PackageReference>`, no `Process`**, the `ISmtpSender` BCL-I/O precedent). **Fail-open by construction** (§2.1): a missing daemon / refused connection / malformed reply / oversize payload / any read or socket fault all degrade to `Clean`, **never a throw, never a false-positive quarantine**. Config: `VirusScanOptions` (`Host`/`Port` default `127.0.0.1:3310` — the `clamd.conf` `TCPSocket` default; `MaxScanBytes` mirror of `StreamMaxLen`). Only a `FOUND …` reply maps to `Infected`; `OK` / `STREAM ERROR` / anything else → `Clean`.
  2. **`src/Kumunita.Core/Media/MediaObject.cs`** — added **`public bool Quarantined { get; set; }`** (default `false`). The `MediaDocTypes.Configure` registration (`opts.Schema.For<MediaObject>()`) is **unchanged** — a new column Marten applies idempotently at boot (ADR 0004 §B; the pin's §3 `MediaDocTypes` GOTCHA honored — no wrong-surface registration).
  3. **`src/Kumunita.Core/DependencyInjection.cs`** — registered the seam next to the existing `IMediaStore`/`IMediaFileStore` block: `services.AddOptions<VirusScanOptions>();` + `services.AddTransient<IVirusScanner, ClamAvScanner>();` (the Core composition root supplies the fail-open default; the F16 owner-ops step is what points it at a live daemon).
  4. **The three serve-lane gates (the Web glue the "serve lane is the enforcement point" invariant requires)** — in **each** of `AttachmentController.Serve` (after the step-2 store-miss 404, before the reverse-lookup), `ContentImageController.Serve` (same point), and `DocumentController.Serve` (after the blob-miss 404, before the `OpenReadAsync` serve): one shared `IsQuarantinedOrInfectedAsync(id, stored, ct)` helper each — (a) `if (stored.Quarantined) return true;` (idempotent short-circuit, **no re-scan**, §3); (b) scan via the serve lane's **own** `IMediaStore.OpenReadAsync` (re-used, **no new byte-read seam**, §8.3) → `scanner.ScanAsync`, wrapped in `try/catch` **fail-open** (a fault degrades to `Clean`, §2.1); (c) on `Infected` → `stored.Quarantined = true` + **one** `SaveChangesAsync` (C3 single-write-lane, ADR 0006) + the caller returns **`NotFound()` (404, zero audit rows, not 403 — the no-leak posture, §4)**; (d) `Clean` → fall through to the existing serve path **unchanged** (the `attachment` + `nosniff` mitigation STAYS, §1 scope fence). Each ctor gained the `IVirusScanner` dependency (the "widen the ctor as the lane needs it" shape). **The upload lane, the `IMediaStore`/`IMediaFileStore` seams, and the F15 lifecycle job are UNTOUCHED.**
- **Drift (recorded, NOT guessed):** **no codebase divergence — no `## F16.U2 — Drift pause`.** One **design reconciliation** (the pin did not anticipate): making `IVirusScanner` a **nullable ctor param with a `null` default** (the repo's established optional-seam idiom — `DocumentController`'s `localization`/`translationProvider`/`userInfo` are all `= null` so test-construction sites keep compiling). The first build failed with **7 `CS7036` errors** in **existing test files** (`AttachmentUploadTests`, `ContentImageUploadTests`, `DocumentControllerTests` ×5) that construct the three controllers directly without a scanner. Rather than editing those test files (out of the ≤3-file budget and F16.U3's concern), the seam was made optional + a **`if (scanner is null) return false;`** fail-open floor added to each helper — a **null scanner = the §2.1 fail-open default** (the serve proceeds unchanged), which is the **correct** behavior, not a workaround. This also means the existing upload/serve tests now exercise the fail-open path (a `Clean`-verdict serve) — **confirmed by the green suite below**. A second, mechanical drift: `Stream` has **no** `ToArray()` (it's `MemoryStream`) and `TcpClient` is `IDisposable`, not `IAsyncDisposable` — both fixed in the first impl pass (the `Stream→MemoryStream` copy + a plain `using`).
- **Test result (per `AGENTS.md` test quirk — `dotnet exec` of the dll, NOT `dotnet test`):**
  - `dotnet build Kumunita.slnx -c Debug` — **Build succeeded, 0 Error(s)** (the exit gate; 18 warnings, all pre-existing test-file nullability/xUnit).
  - **No-regression confirm:** `dotnet exec tests/Kumunita.Web.Tests/bin/Debug/net10.0/Kumunita.Web.Tests.dll` → **`Kumunita.Web.Tests Total: 1074, Errors: 0, Failed: 0, Skipped: 1, Not Run: 0`, EXIT=0** (the 1074 includes the existing upload/serve tests now driving the fail-open gate path). **The F16-pinned test `InfectedUpload_NotServed` is F16.U3, NOT this unit — not present/run here.** `Kumunita.Core.Tests` not run (the seam + flag are exercised by the Web gate; a fresh clean-slate agent runs it for F16.U3's no-regression leg + `docker container prune -f` after).
- **Open items (owner, **recorded, NOT assumed done**):** **run ClamAV on the prod volume** (the `code+ops` half — install `clamd`, point `VirusScan__Host`/`VirusScan__Port` at it, confirm it scans the upload volume). **Until this runs, the serve gate is fail-open (a no-op on a missing daemon)** — the `nosniff` + `attachment` mitigation (which stays) is the active control. (Same owner-ops open item F16.U1 recorded — unchanged.)
- **Invariants held (pin §7):** Core stays HTTP-free (ADR 0006-D — the seam is a pure "bytes in → verdict out" contract, no `HttpClient`; the default impl drives a **local** daemon over a loopback socket — **no `<PackageReference>`**); **the serve lane is the enforcement point** (NOT the upload lane — the upload / `PutAsync` write path, the M24 quota gate, the C-MED·7 orphan-safe ordering are **untouched**); **no new bounded context / no new doc type** (one bool on the existing `MediaObject`, the `MediaDocTypes` registration unchanged; one new seam `IVirusScanner`; three one-line gates); C3 single-write-lane / one-commit (the flag-write is one `SaveChangesAsync`); the no-leak 404 posture (404, not 403, zero audit rows); the existing serve mitigation STAYS (`attachment` + `nosniff` untouched); the fail-open default is recorded, not guessed (a null / missing scanner → `Clean`).
- **F16 is now at U3 (F16.U2 COMPLETE — the `IVirusScanner` seam + the `MediaObject.Quarantined` bool + the three serve-lane gates).** The prod-hardening lane stays **open** — **F16.U3 + F17–F22 remain**. **No 6-member close-flip** (that happens at **F22**, the lane's last unit). Next unit = **F16.U3** (the pinned test **`InfectedUpload_NotServed`**, Web.Tests, pin §6 — a **stub** `IVirusScanner` returning `Infected` drives the `AttachmentController.Serve` lane; assert the 404 + the `Quarantined` flag set + the idempotent short-circuit) — a fresh clean-slate agent. **F16.U3 MUST read the pin's §6 (the pinned test name + the four legs + the stub shape + the `MediaDocTypes` harness GOTCHA at `MediaLifecycleTests.cs:116`) + §7 (the invariants) + the F16.U2 entry above (the seam shape, the `IVirusScanner? scanner = null` optional ctor + the `if (scanner is null) return false;` fail-open floor, and the `IsQuarantinedOrInfectedAsync` helper in each of the three controllers).**

## F16.U3 — the `InfectedUpload_NotServed` pinned test (THIRD AND LAST UNIT OF F16)
- **Landed (exactly ONE new file; **no `src/**` change, no `<PackageReference>`, no migration, no `.slnx` change**):** `tests/Kumunita.Web.Tests/F16U3_ServeScanTests.cs` (**NEW**) — the single pinned test **`InfectedUpload_NotServed`** (the **UNRENAMED** pin §6 name — the next audit run diffs against it) + two private stubs (`InfectedScanner` (call-counting, always `Infected`) + `CleanScanner` (always `Clean`)) + a `PostgresFixture` boot harness. **Test-only code unit.**
- **The four pin §6 legs (all asserted in the one pinned test):**
  1. **the serve lane 404s** — `Assert.IsType<NotFoundResult>(first)` + `Assert.False(first is FileResult)` (the not-served contract, §4 — **not** a 403, **not** a `FileResult`);
  2. **the quarantine flag is set** — `Assert.True((await media.GetAsync(infectedId))!.Quarantined)` (a fresh read shows the §3/§5.4 "persist the flag" step committed);
  3. **idempotent short-circuit** — a second `Serve(infectedId)` returns `NotFoundResult` again and `Assert.Equal(1, infectedScanner.ScanCount)` (the call-counting stub proves the §3 `if (stored.Quarantined) return true;` branch fires **without** re-invoking the scanner);
  4. **(optional control, the F15.U3 keeps-clean leg)** — a **second** byte whose stub returns `Clean` is **served** (`Assert.True(served is FileResult)` — a `FileStreamResult` subclass, the 200-shape) and its `Quarantined` **stays `false`** (the §5.4 "Clean → fall through unchanged" path).
- **Harness (the pin §6 + the F16.U2 GOTCHA, both held):** one `AttachmentController` per scanner, over a **real** `LocalVolumeMediaStore` (a `LocalVolumeFileStore` on a temp dir + a real `DocumentStore` — **NOT** a null-skip) so the step-2 `GetAsync` returns a live `MediaObject` with `Quarantined = false`. The boot store **MUST** (and does) call **`MediaDocTypes.Configure(opts)`** (the §3 GOTCHA — `MediaObject` is on the `MediaDocTypes` surface, not `CoreDocTypes`/`PostDocTypes`) **and `PostDocTypes.Configure(opts)`** (leg-4's real sealed `PostService` reverse-lookup runs over `Post`/`PostReply`, which are on `PostDocTypes`). The quarantine gate fires **before** the step-3 reverse-lookup, so `IAuthorizationService` / `IAnnouncementService` are **NSubstitute stand-ins** (never invoked on the Infected path) and `PostService` is a **real** instance (it is sealed — NSubstitute cannot proxy it) built over `Substitute.For<IUserInfoService>()` + `Substitute.For<IAuthorizationService>()` + the real store. The **non-null** `InfectedScanner` is the point (a **null** scanner is the §2.1 fail-open floor — `if (scanner is null) return false;` — and would **not** drive the `Infected` path; the fail-open/unavailable-daemon path is a **recorded decision, NOT a pinned leg** — no separate test for it, per the pin).
- **Compile fixes (first-pass, both in this file only):** (a) xunit v3 has **no** `Assert.NotType` → leg-1's "not a `FileResult`" leg uses `Assert.False(first is FileResult)`; (b) NSubstitute 6.x uses **`Arg.Any<T>()`**, not `It.IsAny<T>()` → the leg-4 canned `announcements.GetAsync(cleanId, (string?)null, Arg.Any<IReadOnlySet<string>>())` return. (The first `IsType<FileResult>` leg-4 assert would also have **failed at runtime** — `File(stream, type)` returns a concrete `FileStreamResult`, a `FileResult` subclass — so leg-4 uses `Assert.True(served is FileResult)`, the pin's "a `FileResult`/200-shape" wording.)
- **Test result (per `AGENTS.md` test quirk — `dotnet exec` of the dll, NOT `dotnet test`):**
  - `dotnet build Kumunita.slnx -c Debug` — **Build succeeded, 0 Error(s)** (the exit gate; 15 warnings, all pre-existing nullability/xUnit in other test files — none in this file).
  - **`dotnet exec tests/Kumunita.Web.Tests/bin/Debug/net10.0/Kumunita.Web.Tests.dll` → `Kumunita.Web.Tests Total: 1075, Errors: 0, Failed: 0, Skipped: 1, Not Run: 0, Time: 18.041s`** (the +1 over the F16.U2 baseline of **1074** is the new `InfectedUpload_NotServed`; the `Failed: 0` + the +1 count is the confirmation it **ran and passed** — the in-process runner prints only the aggregate on a green suite and does not list passing names; the single `Skipped: 1` is the pre-existing `ImproveHarnessTests.ImproveCheck_Gate_Passes` (`pwsh is not on PATH`) — unchanged). The `--treenode-filter` single-test form is **not** supported by this xunit v3 4.0.2 build (`unknown option`), so the full-dll run is the path (AGENTS.md test quirk). `Kumunita.Core.Tests` not re-run here (F16 is a Web serve-lane test; the seam + flag are exercised by the Web gate — a fresh clean-slate agent runs it for F17's no-regression leg + `docker container prune -f` after). - **Invariants held (pin §7):** Web class → **Web.Tests** precedent (F12.U3 / F9.U4 / F10.U3 direct-controller-construction harness, here over a real `PostgresFixture` store because the quarantine persist needs a real `IDocumentStore`); the pinned name is **not renamed** (`InfectedUpload_NotServed`); the test drives the **`Infected`** path (the fail-open / unavailable path is the recorded §2.1 decision, **not** a pinned leg); the `attachment` + `nosniff` serve mitigation and the `AttachmentController.Serve` lane gate under test are **unchanged** (no `src/**` touch); C3 single-write-lane / one-commit (the gate's `SaveChangesAsync` is what the harness's real store commits); the no-leak 404 posture (404, not 403) asserted, not re-implemented.
- **F16 is now COMPLETE (U1–U3 done).** The prod-hardening lane stays **open** — **F17–F22 remain**. **No 6-member close-flip** (that happens at **F22**, the lane's last unit). Next unit = **F17.U1** (the MAIL-03 bounce/complaint suppression Part-0 design pin) — a fresh clean-slate agent.

- **Landed (exactly ONE new doc file; **no `src/**` change, no `<PackageReference>`, no migration, no `.slnx` change**):** `docs/design/prod-hardening-f17-bounce.md` (**NEW**, §1–§7). No business-key index (the `Id` IS the email — the `MediaObject` precedent). **No `Program.cs` change** — `NotificationDocTypes.Configure(opts)` is already called in the `Program.cs` boot block.
  2. **The gate insertion point** — `NotificationService.EmitAsync` (the ADR 0084 overload), a **new step (5c)** inserted **immediately before the `await _mailer.StageAsync(...)` call at `NotificationService.cs:361`** (the sole `StageAsync` in that method). A **read-only** `session.LoadAsync<SuppressedAddress>(profile.Email.Trim().ToLowerInvariant(), ct)` → `return notification` on a hit (the inbox row is already stored — D7; only the email is skipped). **No new audit row** (the ADR 0078 sample-suppression gate at `NotificationService.cs:~238` and the ADR 0084 per-target gate at step 0 are **untouched**; F17 is additive). The gate is a **Core** read — Core stays HTTP-free (pin §2).
  3. **The provider-callback ingest** — route **`POST /api/mail/suppress`** (pin §3.1), JSON body `{ email, reason: "bounce"|"complaint", source: <provider> }`, `204`/`400` responses, **shared-secret header** auth (`MailSuppression__IngestToken` config key — the `EscalationToken`/`GuestAccess` token-seam precedent, not an Identity credential). A new `ISuppressionService` / `SuppressionService` in `Kumunita.Core.Notifications` (the `SuppressAsync` + `IsSuppressedAsync` shape, pin §3.2) + a Web `MailSuppressionController` (pin §3.3). **Idempotency: last-write-wins** — a `Store` on an existing `Id` overwrites `Reason`/`Created`/`Source` (pin §3.4). **Core stays HTTP-free (ADR 0006-D):** the service is Core, the controller is Web.
  4. **The pinned test** — **`SuppressedAddress_NotMailed`** (the **UNRENAMED** pin §4 name — the next audit run diffs against it). A **Core** test (`tests/Kumunita.Core.Tests/F17U3_SuppressionGateTests.cs`, U3 creates) over a real `PostgresFixture` store (the boot store **MUST** call `NotificationDocTypes.Configure(opts)` — the F16.U3 GOTCHA-1 precedent) + a **recording `IMailerStage` stub** (counts `StageAsync` calls, stores nothing) + a real (sealed) `NotificationService` over NSubstitute `IUserInfoService`/`ITranslationProvider` + `SuppressForSampleAccountsInProduction = false` (the ADR 0078 gate is disabled — the test drives the **F17** gate). **Three legs (pin §4.2):** (1) `Assert.Equal(0, mailer.StageAsyncCount)` (the email is NOT staged); (2) the inbox row **IS** stored (query by `IdempotencyKey`); (3) **no `AccessAudit` row** is written (the gate is a read-only lookup). A `SuppressedAddress_NotPresent_EmailStillSent` control test is **optional** (U3's choice — the pin pins the one UNRENAMED name).
  5. **The invariants (pin §5, the 10-row table):** Core stays HTTP-free (ADR 0006-D); C3 single-write-lane (one `SaveChangesAsync` for the `SuppressedAddress` write; the gate is a read); the existing per-recipient opt-out (ADR 0078 + ADR 0084 gates) is **unchanged** (F17 is additive, not a re-shape); the mail-out path's existing error handling is **untouched** (the `StageAsync` call site + `OutboxEmailStager` + `SmtpSender` are unmodified); the pinned test name is not a placeholder; the owner-ops step is recorded, not assumed; server-rendered stack (no SPA); audit-by-default (the gate writes no audit row — the `SuppressedAddress` row is the record); no new bounded context; no `<PackageReference>`/migration/`.slnx` change.
